              <span id="dvd-toggle-knob" style="position:absolute;top:2px;left:2px;width:18px;height:18px;background:#555;border-radius:50%;transition:transform .3s,background .3s,box-shadow .3s;"></span>
            </label>
            <span id="dvd-toggle-label" style="font-family:var(--font-mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:#555;letter-spacing:0.08em;min-width:24px;">OFF</span>
          </div>
          <script>
          (function() {
            var cb = document.getElementById('dvd-bouncer-toggle');
            var knob = document.getElementById('dvd-toggle-knob');
            var label = document.getElementById('dvd-toggle-label');
            if (!cb) return;
            cb.addEventListener('change', function() {
              if (window.DVDBouncer) window.DVDBouncer.toggle();
              var on = cb.checked;
              knob.style.transform = on ? 'translateX(20px)' : 'translateX(0)';
              knob.style.background = on ? '#00f5ff' : '#555';
              knob.style.boxShadow = on ? '0 0 8px rgba(0,245,255,0.6)' : 'none';
              knob.parentElement.previousElementSibling.style.background = on ? 'rgba(0,245,255,0.18)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
              knob.parentElement.previousElementSibling.style.borderColor = on ? 'rgba(0,245,255,0.6)' : 'rgba(0,245,255,0.3)';
              label.textContent = on ? 'ON' : 'OFF';
              label.style.color = on ? '#00f5ff' : '#555';
            });
          })();
          </script>
          <div style="margin-top:1rem;">

    <img src="<?php echo get_template_directory_uri(); ?>/assets/images/affiche.png" alt="Affiche du groupe JoyStick FM" style="max-width:100%; max-height:80vh; border:2px solid var(--bleu-neon); border-radius:var(--radius); box-shadow:var(--glow-bleu); object-fit:contain;">

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  /* ── Popup affiche ── */
  const btnAffiche  = document.getElementById('btn-affiche-groupe');
  const popupAffiche = document.getElementById('popup-affiche');
  const closeAffiche = document.getElementById('close-affiche');

  if (btnAffiche && popupAffiche && closeAffiche) {
    btnAffiche.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      popupAffiche.style.display = 'flex';
      setTimeout(() => { popupAffiche.style.opacity = '1'; }, 10);
    });
    closeAffiche.addEventListener('click', () => {
      popupAffiche.style.opacity = '0';
      setTimeout(() => { popupAffiche.style.display = 'none'; }, 300);
    });
    popupAffiche.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === popupAffiche) {
        popupAffiche.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => { popupAffiche.style.display = 'none'; }, 300);
      }
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && popupAffiche.style.display === 'flex') {
        popupAffiche.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => { popupAffiche.style.display = 'none'; }, 300);
      }
    });
  }

  /* ── Compteur d'auditeurs réel via proxy Icecast ── */
  const statEl = document.getElementById('stat-listeners');
  if (statEl) {
    const proxyUrl = window.THEME_URI + '/assets/php/icecast-proxy.php';
    function fetchListeners() {
      fetch(proxyUrl + '?t=' + Date.now())
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => {
          statEl.textContent = d.online ? d.listeners : '—';
        })
        .catch(() => { statEl.textContent = '—'; });
    }
    fetchListeners();
    setInterval(fetchListeners, 10000); /* Mise à jour toutes les 10 s sur la home */
  }
});
</script>
<?php get_footer(); ?>

<?php
/**
 * JoyStick FM — functions.php
 * Fonctions WordPress du thème sur-mesure
 */

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 1. SUPPORT DU THÈME
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function joystickfm_setup()
{
    add_theme_support('title-tag');
    add_theme_support('post-thumbnails');
    add_theme_support('html5', ['search-form', 'comment-form', 'comment-list', 'gallery', 'caption']);
}
add_action('after_setup_theme', 'joystickfm_setup');

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 2. ENQUEUE DES SCRIPTS ET STYLES (CENTRALISÉ)
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function joystickfm_enqueue()
{
    $v = '6.0.' . time(); // Utilisation de time() pour forcer le rafraîchissement du cache pendant tes tests

    // --- 1. LES STYLES (CSS & FONTS) ---
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-fonts-pixel', 'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Press+Start+2P&family=Share+Tech+Mono&display=swap', [], null);
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-style', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/css/style.css', [], $v);
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-animations', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/css/animations.css', ['jfm-style'], $v);
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-responsive', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/css/responsive.css', ['jfm-style'], $v);
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-xpbar', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/css/xp-bar-styles.css', ['jfm-style'], $v);

    // Le CSS du Chat
    wp_enqueue_style('jfm-chat-style', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/css/chat.css', [], $v);

    // --- 2. FORCER JQUERY ---
    wp_enqueue_script('jquery');

    // --- 3. LES SCRIPTS (JS) ---
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-launch-game', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/joystick-launch-game.js', [], $v, true);
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-easter-eggs', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/easter-eggs.js', ['jfm-launch-game'], $v, true);
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-radio-player', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/radio-player.js', ['jfm-easter-eggs'], $v, true);
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-main', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/main.js', ['jfm-radio-player'], $v, true);
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-dvd-bouncer', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/dvd-bouncer.js', ['jfm-easter-eggs'], $v, true);

    if (is_page('contact')) {
        wp_enqueue_script('jfm-form', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/form-validation.js', [], $v, true);
    }

    // Le JS du Chat (dépend de jquery)
    wp_enqueue_script('jfm-chat-script', get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/js/chat.js', array('jquery'), $v, true);

    // --- 4. LES VARIABLES GLOBALES (Localize) ---
    wp_localize_script('jfm-radio-player', 'JFM_CONFIG', [
        'theme_uri' => get_template_directory_uri(),
        'icecast_proxy' => get_template_directory_uri() . '/assets/php/icecast-proxy.php',
        'ajaxurl' => admin_url('admin-ajax.php'),
        'home_url' => home_url('/'),
    ]);

    wp_localize_script('jfm-chat-script', 'JFM_CHAT', [
        'ajax_url' => admin_url('admin-ajax.php'),
        'nonce' => wp_create_nonce('jfm_chat_nonce'),
        'upload_size' => 4,
        'is_admin' => current_user_can('manage_options'), // ← Ligne à ajouter
    ]);
}
add_action('wp_enqueue_scripts', 'joystickfm_enqueue');


/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 3. CRÉATION DE LA TABLE CHAT
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function joystickfm_create_chat_table()
{
    global $wpdb;
    $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'jfm_chat_messages';
    $charset = $wpdb->get_charset_collate();

    // Ne recrée pas si elle existe déjà
    if ($wpdb->get_var("SHOW TABLES LIKE '{$table}'") === $table) {
        return;
    }

    $sql = "CREATE TABLE {$table} (
        id          BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
        username    VARCHAR(50)     NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Anonyme',
        message     TEXT,
        type        ENUM('text','image','gif','mixed') NOT NULL DEFAULT 'text',
        file_url    VARCHAR(500)    DEFAULT NULL,
        ip_hash     VARCHAR(64)     NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
        created_at  DATETIME        NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
        PRIMARY KEY (id),
        INDEX idx_created (created_at)
    ) {$charset};";

    require_once ABSPATH . 'wp-admin/includes/upgrade.php';
    dbDelta($sql);
}
// Crée la table à chaque activation du thème
add_action('after_switch_theme', 'joystickfm_create_chat_table');
// Crée aussi si elle n'existe pas encore (sécurité)
add_action('init', function () {
    if (!get_option('jfm_chat_table_created')) {
        joystickfm_create_chat_table();
        global $wpdb;
        $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'jfm_chat_messages';
        if ($wpdb->get_var("SHOW TABLES LIKE '{$table}'") === $table) {
            update_option('jfm_chat_table_created', '1');
        }
    }
});

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 4. INCLUSION DES HANDLERS PHP CHAT
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
require_once get_template_directory() . '/assets/php/chat-handler.php';
require_once get_template_directory() . '/assets/php/upload-handler.php';

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 5. PROXY ICECAST VIA AJAX WORDPRESS
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function joystickfm_icecast_status()
{
    $icecast_url = 'http://10.10.10.12:8000/status-json.xsl';
    $response = wp_remote_get($icecast_url, ['timeout' => 3, 'sslverify' => false]);

    if (is_wp_error($response)) {
        wp_send_json(['online' => false, 'listeners' => 0, 'title' => 'JoyStick FM', 'artist' => '—']);
        return;
    }

    $body = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
    $data = json_decode($body, true);

    if (!$data || !isset($data['icestats'])) {
        wp_send_json(['online' => false, 'listeners' => 0, 'title' => 'JoyStick FM', 'artist' => '—']);
        return;
    }

    $icestats = $data['icestats'];
    $source = $icestats['source'] ?? null;

    if (isset($source['listenurl']))
        $source = [$source];

    $found = null;
    if (is_array($source)) {
        foreach ($source as $s) {
            if (strpos($s['listenurl'] ?? '', 'joystick-fm') !== false) {
                $found = $s;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!$found)
            $found = $source[0] ?? null;
    }

    if (!$found) {
        wp_send_json(['online' => false, 'listeners' => 0, 'title' => 'JoyStick FM — Hors ligne', 'artist' => '—']);
        return;
    }

    $listeners = (int)($found['listeners'] ?? 0);
    $rawTitle = trim($found['title'] ?? '');
    $rawArtist = trim($found['artist'] ?? '');
    $title = $artist = '—';

    if ($rawArtist && $rawTitle) {
        $artist = $rawArtist;
        $title = $rawTitle;
    }
    elseif ($rawTitle && strpos($rawTitle, ' - ') !== false) {
        [$artist, $title] = explode(' - ', $rawTitle, 2);
    }
    else {
        $title = $rawTitle ?: 'JoyStick FM';
        $artist = 'JoyStick FM';
    }

    wp_send_json(['online' => true, 'listeners' => $listeners, 'title' => trim($title), 'artist' => trim($artist)]);
}
add_action('wp_ajax_nopriv_icecast_status', 'joystickfm_icecast_status');
add_action('wp_ajax_icecast_status', 'joystickfm_icecast_status');

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 6. BARRE D'ADMIN / SÉCURITÉ
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
add_filter('show_admin_bar', '__return_false');
remove_action('wp_head', 'wp_generator');

function joystickfm_wp_title($title, $sep)
{
    if (is_feed())
        return $title;
    global $page, $paged;
    $title .= get_bloginfo('name', 'display');
    $site_description = get_bloginfo('description', 'display');
    if ($site_description && (is_home() || is_front_page()))
        $title .= " $sep $site_description";
    return $title;
}
add_filter('wp_title', 'joystickfm_wp_title', 10, 2);

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════
 7. TÂCHE CRON : ARCHIVAGE ET NETTOYAGE DU CHAT
 ══════════════════════════════════════════════ */
// 1. Planification de la tâche toutes les 24h
if (!wp_next_scheduled('jfm_daily_chat_archive_event')) {
    wp_schedule_event(time(), 'daily', 'jfm_daily_chat_archive_event');
}
add_action('jfm_daily_chat_archive_event', 'joystickfm_archive_chat');

// 2. La fonction d'archivage (La "Garbage Collection")
function joystickfm_archive_chat() {
    global $wpdb;
    $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'jfm_chat_messages';
    
    // Récupérer tous les messages
    $messages = $wpdb->get_results("SELECT * FROM {$table} ORDER BY created_at ASC");
    if (empty($messages)) return;

    $upload_dir = wp_upload_dir();
    $archive_dir = $upload_dir['basedir'] . '/chat_archives';
    
    // Créer le dossier d'archives s'il n'existe pas
    if (!file_exists($archive_dir)) {
        wp_mkdir_p($archive_dir);
    }

    $date_str = current_time('Y-m-d_H-i');
    $zip_path = $archive_dir . '/chat_archive_' . $date_str . '.zip';
    $txt_log = "=== ARCHIVE JOYSTICK FM CHAT - " . current_time('d/m/Y') . " ===\r\n\r\n";

    // Utilisation de la classe native de PHP pour compresser
    $zip = new ZipArchive();
    if ($zip->open($zip_path, ZipArchive::CREATE | ZipArchive::OVERWRITE) === true) {
        
        foreach ($messages as $m) {
            $txt_log .= "[{$m->created_at}] {$m->username} : {$m->message}\r\n";
            
            // Si le message a une pièce jointe
            if (!empty($m->file_url)) {
                // Convertir l'URL web en chemin absolu sur le serveur Debian
                $local_path = str_replace($upload_dir['baseurl'], $upload_dir['basedir'], $m->file_url);
                
                if (file_exists($local_path)) {
                    $txt_log .= "   -> Pièce jointe archivée : " . basename($local_path) . "\r\n";
                    // Ajouter l'image dans le ZIP (dans un sous-dossier medias/)
                    $zip->addFile($local_path, 'medias/' . basename($local_path));
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Ajouter le fichier texte de la conversation dans le ZIP
        $zip->addFromString('conversation_' . $date_str . '.txt', $txt_log);
        $zip->close();

        // 3. NETTOYAGE : Suppression physique des images originelles
        foreach ($messages as $m) {
            if (!empty($m->file_url)) {
                $local_path = str_replace($upload_dir['baseurl'], $upload_dir['basedir'], $m->file_url);
                if (file_exists($local_path)) {
                    unlink($local_path); // Supprime le fichier du disque
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. NETTOYAGE SQL : Remise à zéro de la table (ID repart à 1)
        $wpdb->query("TRUNCATE TABLE {$table}");
    }
}

// ASTUCE DE DEBUG : Permet de déclencher l'archivage manuellement pour tester
if (isset($_GET['force_archive']) && current_user_can('manage_options')) {
    add_action('init', function() {
        joystickfm_archive_chat();
        die('Archivage forcé et terminé ! Regardez dans wp-content/uploads/chat_archives/');
    });
}

<script>
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   Variables CSS nécessaires pour le contenu modal
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
window.THEME_URI = '';

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   KONAMI DEMO (standalone sans easter-eggs.js complet)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const KONAMI = ['ArrowUp','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','b','a'];
const ARROWS = ['↑','↑','↓','↓','←','→','←','→','B','A'];
let kidx = 0;
let kResetTimer = null;

// Construire les touches
const krow = document.getElementById('krow');
ARROWS.forEach((ic,i) => {
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'kk'; d.id = 'kk'+i; d.textContent = ic;
  krow.appendChild(d);
});

// Footer hint
const footerHint = document.getElementById('footer-hint');
footerHint.innerHTML = ARROWS.map(a => `<span>${a}</span>`).join(' ') + ' 🎮';

function updateKonamiHint() {
  const spans = footerHint.querySelectorAll('span');
  spans.forEach((sp, i) => {
    sp.style.color = i < kidx ? 'var(--bleu-neon)' : '#2a2a2a';
    sp.style.textShadow = i < kidx ? 'var(--glow-bleu)' : 'none';
  });
}

function flashKey(i, ok) {
  const el = document.getElementById('kk'+i);
  if (!el) return;
  el.classList.add('flash');
  setTimeout(() => { el.classList.remove('flash'); if (ok) el.classList.add('done'); }, 250);
}

function resetKonami() {
  for(let i=0;i<kidx;i++){
    const el=document.getElementById('kk'+i);
    if(el){el.className='kk wrong';setTimeout(()=>el.className='kk',600);}
  }
  kidx = 0;
  updateKonamiHint();
  document.getElementById('lerr').classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('lerr').classList.remove('show'),1800);
}

document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const k = ['b','a'].includes(e.key.toLowerCase()) ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key;
  if (k === KONAMI[kidx]) {
    flashKey(kidx, true);
    kidx++;
    updateKonamiHint();
    clearTimeout(kResetTimer);
    kResetTimer = setTimeout(() => { kidx = 0; updateKonamiHint(); }, 3000);
    if (kidx === KONAMI.length) {
      clearTimeout(kResetTimer);
      setTimeout(successKonami, 250);
    }
  } else {
    if (kidx > 0) resetKonami();
    if (k === KONAMI[0]) {
      kidx = 1;
      flashKey(0, true);
      updateKonamiHint();
      kResetTimer = setTimeout(() => { kidx = 0; updateKonamiHint(); }, 3000);
    }
  }
});

/* Modal helpers */
let _EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false;
window._EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false;

function _modal(html, onClose) {
  if (_EGG_MODAL_OPEN) return;
  _EGG_MODAL_OPEN = true;
  window._EGG_MODAL_OPEN = true;
  const modal = document.getElementById('easter-egg-modal');
  const body  = document.getElementById('easter-egg-content');
  body.innerHTML = html;
  modal.classList.add('show');

  // XP update demo
  const xpFill = document.querySelector('.egg-xp-fill');
  const xpCount = document.querySelector('.egg-xp-count');
  const xpPct   = document.querySelector('.egg-xp-pct');
  if(xpFill){xpFill.style.width='6%';xpFill.classList.add('xp-flash');}
  if(xpCount) xpCount.textContent = '1';
  if(xpPct)   xpPct.textContent   = '6%';

  function close() {
    modal.classList.remove('show');
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
    _EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false;
    window._EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false;
    if (typeof onClose === 'function') onClose();
  }
  function onEsc(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') close(); }
  document.getElementById('egg-close-btn').addEventListener('click', close, { once: true });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
}

function successKonami() {
  _modal(`
    <div style="font-size:3.5rem;margin-bottom:.75rem;animation:eggPop .4s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)">🕹️</div>
    <h2 style="font-family:var(--font-pixel);font-size:1.6rem;color:#FFD700;
               text-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(255,215,0,.5);margin-bottom:.75rem">KONAMI CODE VALIDÉ !</h2>
    <p style="color:var(--texte);font-size:.82rem;margin-bottom:.75rem;line-height:1.7">
      Le code de triche le plus célèbre de l'histoire du jeu vidéo.<br>
      Gradius, Contra, Metal Gear... tu connais tes classiques.
    </p>
    <p style="color:var(--bleu-neon);font-size:.72rem;font-family:var(--font-pixel);margin-bottom:1.4rem">+ 30 Vies ajoutées 👾</p>
    <button id="jfm-play-game-btn" style="
      display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.55rem;
      font-family:var(--font-pixel);font-size:.78rem;
      color:#000;background:#ffe600;
      border:none;border-radius:8px;
      padding:.8rem 1.6rem;cursor:pointer;
      box-shadow:0 0 24px rgba(255,230,0,.6),0 0 50px rgba(255,230,0,.2);
      transition:transform .15s,box-shadow .15s;
      letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;
      animation:eggPop .5s .1s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1) both;">
      🎮 JOUER AU JEU SECRET
    </button>
    <p style="color:var(--texte-dim);font-size:.6rem;font-family:var(--font-pixel);margin-top:.8rem;opacity:.7">
      Ferme la pop-up pour rester sur le site
    </p>`,
  () => { /* nothing */ });

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('jfm-play-game-btn');
    if (!btn) return;
    btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
      btn.style.transform = 'scale(1.07) translateY(-2px)';
      btn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 32px rgba(255,230,0,.9),0 0 70px rgba(255,230,0,.3)';
    });
    btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
      btn.style.transform = '';
      btn.style.boxShadow = '0 0 24px rgba(255,230,0,.6),0 0 50px rgba(255,230,0,.2)';
    });
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const modal = document.getElementById('easter-egg-modal');
      if (modal) modal.classList.remove('show');
      _EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false; window._EGG_MODAL_OPEN = false;
      if (window.JFM_GAME) window.JFM_GAME.openGameOverlay();
    });
  });
}

document.getElementById('demo-open-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (window.JFM_GAME) window.JFM_GAME.openGameOverlay();
});

<!-- ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
     ║  JOYSTICK LAUNCH GAME — v2.0                        ║
     ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝ -->
<script>
(function () {
'use strict';

const GAME_STYLES = `
#jfm-game-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 100000;
  background: #000;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  opacity: 0;
  transition: opacity .4s ease;
  font-family: 'Press Start 2P', monospace;
}
#jfm-game-overlay.visible { opacity: 1; }
#jfm-game-close {
  position: absolute;
  top: 16px; right: 20px;
  z-index: 10;
  background: rgba(255,68,136,.12);
  border: 1px solid rgba(255,68,136,.5);
  color: #ff4488;
  font-family: 'Press Start 2P', monospace;
  font-size: .5rem;
  padding: 8px 14px;
  cursor: pointer;
  letter-spacing: .1em;
  transition: background .2s, box-shadow .2s;
}
#jfm-game-close:hover {
  background: rgba(255,68,136,.25);
  box-shadow: 0 0 16px rgba(255,68,136,.4);
}
#jfm-game-canvas {
  display: block;
  max-width: 100vw;
  max-height: 100vh;
}
`;

function createOverlay() {
  if (document.getElementById('jfm-game-overlay')) return;
  const st = document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent = GAME_STYLES;
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  const overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.id = 'jfm-game-overlay';
  const closeBtn = document.createElement('button');
  closeBtn.id = 'jfm-game-close';
  closeBtn.textContent = '✕ QUITTER (ESC)';
  closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeGameOverlay);
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.id = 'jfm-game-canvas';
  overlay.appendChild(closeBtn);
  overlay.appendChild(canvas);
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('jfm-game-overlay')?.classList.contains('visible')) {
      closeGameOverlay();
    }
  });
}

function openGameOverlay() {
  createOverlay();
  const overlay = document.getElementById('jfm-game-overlay');
  overlay.style.display = 'flex';
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    overlay.classList.add('visible');
    initGame();
  });
}

function closeGameOverlay() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('jfm-game-overlay');
  if (!overlay) return;
  overlay.classList.remove('visible');
  stopGame();
  setTimeout(() => { overlay.style.display = 'none'; }, 400);
}

// ── GAME STATE ──
let canvas, ctx, W, H, GY;
let rafId = null, gameRunning = false;
let state = 'menu';
let chargeValue = 0, chargeDir = 1;
let selChar = 0, score = 0, best = 0, camX = 0, frame = 0;
let particles = [], trails = [], terrain = [], flowerPosX = [];
let diveCooldown = 0, isDiving = false, deathTimer = 0;
const ch = { x:0, y:0, vx:0, vy:0, rot:0, angV:0 };

const CHARS = [
  { name:'CTRL PAD',  col:'#00f0ff', acc:'#003344', mass:1.0, aero:1.0,  elas:.65, size:22, locked:false, desc:'Équilibré · Idéal pour débuter' },
  { name:'JOY STICK', col:'#ffe600', acc:'#333300', mass:.7,  aero:1.4,  elas:.50, size:18, locked:false, desc:'Aérodynamique · Conserve vitesse' },
  { name:'MEGA PAD',  col:'#00ff88', acc:'#003322', mass:1.8, aero:.65,  elas:.85, size:30, locked:false, desc:'Lourd · Rebonds dévastateurs' },
  { name:'RETRO X',   col:'#ff4488', acc:'#440022', mass:1.2, aero:1.1,  elas:.72, size:24, locked:false, desc:'Hybride · Trajectoires variées' },
  { name:'HEADSET',   col:'#bb44ff', acc:'#220044', mass:.9,  aero:1.3,  elas:.74, size:20, locked:true,  desc:'Débloqué à 500m' },
  { name:'ARCADE',    col:'#ff8800', acc:'#331100', mass:1.4, aero:.85,  elas:.80, size:26, locked:true,  desc:'Débloqué à 1000m' },
];

const PIV_X = 160, ARM_L = 72, PIV_Y_OFF = 72;
function getPivY() { return GY - PIV_Y_OFF; }

function resize() {
  if (!canvas) return;
  W = canvas.width  = Math.min(window.innerWidth,  1400);
  H = canvas.height = Math.min(window.innerHeight, 860);
  GY = H - 100;
}

function genTerrain(sx, n) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = sx + i*(200+Math.random()*260);
    const r = Math.random();
    if (r < .28) {
      const h = 48+Math.random()*32, w = h*1.5;
      terrain.push({type:'shroom',x,y:GY-h,w,h,pow:1.2+Math.random()*.6,hit:false});
    } else if (r < .42) {
      terrain.push({type:'cloud',x:x+Math.random()*100,y:80+Math.random()*(H*.28),w:120+Math.random()*80,h:30,hit:false});
    } else if (r < .56) {
      terrain.push({type:'cannon',x,y:GY-60,w:54,h:60,hit:false});
    } else if (r < .68) {
      terrain.push({type:'fly',x:x+Math.random()*60,y:GY-(70+Math.random()*100),w:72,h:18,hit:false});
    } else if (r < .78) {
      terrain.push({type:'mud',x,y:GY-16,w:90+Math.random()*60,h:18});
    } else {
      const h=36+Math.random()*24;
      terrain.push({type:'shroom',x,y:GY-h,w:h*1.4,h,pow:1.5,hit:false});
    }
  }
}

function physics() {
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  const GRAVITY = 0.52*c.mass, DRAG = 1-(0.0015/c.aero), DIVE_BOOST = 1.2+c.mass*.4;
  ch.vy += GRAVITY; ch.vx *= DRAG;
  if (isDiving) ch.vy += DIVE_BOOST;
  ch.x += ch.vx; ch.y += ch.vy;
  ch.rot += ch.angV; ch.angV *= .97;
  camX += (ch.x - W*.3 - camX)*.08;
  score = Math.max(score, Math.max(0, Math.floor((ch.x-280)/2.5)));
  const sz = c.size;
  if (ch.y >= GY-sz) {
    ch.y = GY-sz;
    const mud = terrain.find(t => t.type==='mud' && ch.x>t.x && ch.x<t.x+t.w);
    if (mud) { ch.vx=0;ch.vy=0;spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#774411',22);state='dead';deathTimer=140;return; }
    const bounce = Math.abs(ch.vy)*c.elas*(isDiving?1.8:1);
    ch.vy = -bounce; ch.vx *= .78; ch.angV = (Math.random()-.5)*.4;
    if (bounce > 2) spawnTrail(ch.x, GY);
    if (bounce < 1.8 && Math.abs(ch.vx) < 1.2) { state='dead';deathTimer=140;flowerPosX.push(ch.x); }
  }
  for (const t of terrain) {
    if (t.hit) continue;
    const inX = ch.x+sz > t.x-4 && ch.x-sz < t.x+t.w+4;
    if (!inX) continue;
    if (t.type==='shroom' && ch.y+sz>=t.y && ch.y+sz<=t.y+t.h*.5 && ch.vy>0) {
      const dm = isDiving?2.4:1;
      ch.vy = -Math.abs(ch.vy)*c.elas*t.pow*dm*1.8;
      ch.vx *= 1.06; ch.y = t.y-sz; ch.angV=(Math.random()-.5)*.65;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff66ee',18);t.hit=true;setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,600);
    } else if (t.type==='cannon' && ch.x>t.x+4 && ch.x<t.x+t.w-4 && ch.y+sz>=t.y+8 && ch.y<=t.y+t.h) {
      ch.vx=Math.max(Math.abs(ch.vx),18)*2.0;ch.vy=-6;ch.y=t.y-sz;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff8800',22);t.hit=true;setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,800);
    } else if (t.type==='fly' && ch.y+sz>=t.y && ch.y<=t.y+t.h+6 && ch.vy>0) {
      ch.vy=-20;ch.vx*=.9;ch.y=t.y-sz;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff44ff',14);t.hit=true;setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,400);
    } else if (t.type==='cloud' && ch.x>t.x && ch.x<t.x+t.w && ch.y+sz>=t.y && ch.y<=t.y+t.h+8 && ch.vy>0) {
      ch.vy=-Math.abs(ch.vy)*.88;ch.y=t.y-sz;spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#aae0ff',8);
    }
  }
  if (diveCooldown > 0) diveCooldown--;
  if (ch.vy < 0) isDiving = false;
  const maxT = terrain.length ? Math.max(...terrain.map(t=>t.x)) : 0;
  if (ch.x+W > maxT-400) genTerrain(maxT+200, 12);
  if (score >= 500) CHARS[4].locked = false;
  if (score >= 1000) CHARS[5].locked = false;
}

function spawnP(x,y,col,n){for(let i=0;i<n;i++)particles.push({x,y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*9,vy:-Math.random()*9-1,col,life:1,s:3+Math.random()*5});}
function spawnTrail(x,y){trails.push({x,y:y-4,life:1});}
function tickP(){
  for(let i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=particles[i];p.x+=p.vx;p.y+=p.vy;p.vy+=.24;p.life-=.025;if(p.life<=0)particles.splice(i,1);}
  for(let i=trails.length-1;i>=0;i--){trails[i].life-=.003;if(trails[i].life<=0)trails.splice(i,1);}
}

function getArmAngle() {
  if (state==='menu'||state==='ready') return -Math.PI*.5;
  if (state==='charging') return -Math.PI*.5+chargeValue*Math.PI*.9;
  return -Math.PI*1.0;
}
function getCubePos() {
  const a=getArmAngle(), px=PIV_X-camX, py=getPivY();
  return {x:px+Math.cos(a)*ARM_L, y:py+Math.sin(a)*ARM_L};
}

function doLaunch() {
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  const ang = Math.PI*.44 - chargeValue*Math.PI*.28;
  const spd = 14+chargeValue*24;
  const a = -Math.PI*.5+chargeValue*Math.PI*.9;
  ch.x = PIV_X+Math.cos(a)*ARM_L; ch.y = getPivY()+Math.sin(a)*ARM_L;
  ch.vx = Math.cos(-ang)*spd/c.mass; ch.vy = -Math.sin(-ang)*spd/c.mass;
  ch.rot=0; ch.angV=(Math.random()-.5)*.45;
  score=0; diveCooldown=0; isDiving=false; state='flying'; camX=0;
}

function reset() {
  chargeValue=0;chargeDir=1;isDiving=false;score=0;camX=0;
  diveCooldown=0;particles=[];trails=[];terrain=[];flowerPosX=[];
  ch.x=PIV_X;ch.y=getPivY()-ARM_L-CHARS[selChar].size;
  ch.vx=0;ch.vy=0;ch.rot=0;ch.angV=0;
  genTerrain(350,40);state='menu';
}

// DRAWING
function drawBg() {
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#03000f');g.addColorStop(.6,'#060020');g.addColorStop(1,'#0a0030');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  ctx.save();
  for(let i=0;i<110;i++){
    const sx=(i*137.508)%W,sy=(i*97.33)%(H*.5);
    const br=(.4+.6*Math.sin(frame*.018+i*.9))*.9;
    ctx.globalAlpha=br;ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,i%5===0?2:1,i%5===0?2:1);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.restore();
  const gs=100,ox=(camX*.25)%gs;
  ctx.save();
  for(let r=0;r<8;r++){const y=GY-r*30;ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(100,0,220,${.04+r*.035})`;ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(W,y);ctx.stroke();}
  for(let x=-ox;x<W+gs;x+=gs){ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,0,130,.2)';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,GY-240);ctx.lineTo(x-55,GY);ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.restore();
  const cOff=camX*.12;
  for(let i=0;i<26;i++){
    const bx=((i*179+40)%1600-cOff%1600+1600)%1600-120;
    const bh=40+(i*53)%100,bw=20+(i*31)%42;
    ctx.fillStyle=i%2===0?'rgba(0,50,140,.5)':'rgba(50,0,100,.5)';
    ctx.fillRect(bx,GY-200-bh,bw,bh);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,0,.3)';
    for(let wy=bh-8;wy>8;wy-=12){if((i+wy)%3!==0)continue;for(let wx=3;wx<bw-3;wx+=8)ctx.fillRect(bx+wx,GY-200-bh+wy,4,6);}
  }
}

function drawGround(){
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,GY-6,0,H);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#1a0060');g.addColorStop(.05,'#0d0038');g.addColorStop(1,'#04000f');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,GY-6,W,H-GY+6);
  ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#9900ff';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.fillStyle='#cc66ff';ctx.fillRect(0,GY-3,W,3);ctx.restore();
  for(const fx of flowerPosX){
    const sx=fx-camX;if(sx<-60||sx>W+60)continue;
    ctx.save();ctx.font='18px serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('🌸',sx,GY-2);ctx.restore();
  }
}

function drawTrails(){
  for(const t of trails){
    const cx=t.x-camX;ctx.globalAlpha=t.life*.9;
    const hue=(t.x*.4)%360;ctx.fillStyle=`hsl(${hue},100%,70%)`;
    ctx.fillRect(cx-4,t.y,8,2);ctx.fillRect(cx-1,t.y-4,2,10);
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(cx-1,t.y-1,2,2);ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
}

function drawTerrain(){
  for(const t of terrain){
    const tx=t.x-camX;if(tx<-300||tx>W+300)continue;
    if(t.type==='shroom'){
      ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#ff44ee';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?40:12;
      const stemW=t.w*.28,stemH=t.h*.48;
      ctx.fillStyle='#ccaacc';ctx.fillRect(tx+t.w*.5-stemW/2,GY-stemH,stemW,stemH);
      ctx.fillStyle=t.hit?'#fff':'#ee66dd';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(tx+t.w*.5,GY-stemH,t.w*.5,t.h*.62,0,Math.PI,0);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle=t.hit?'#ee66dd':'#fff';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.5-t.w*.16,GY-stemH-t.h*.3,t.w*.08,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.5+t.w*.12,GY-stemH-t.h*.2,t.w*.07,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.5-t.w*.02,GY-stemH-t.h*.5,t.w*.055,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    else if(t.type==='cloud'){
      ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#88ccff';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?25:10;
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(140,195,255,.75)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      roundRect(ctx,tx,t.y,t.w,t.h,15);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.45)';ctx.font='10px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('~FM~',tx+t.w/2,t.y+t.h/2+4);ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
    }
    else if(t.type==='cannon'){
      ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#ff8800';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?35:14;
      const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(tx,0,tx+t.w,0);
      grad.addColorStop(0,'#221100');grad.addColorStop(.5,'#ff7700');grad.addColorStop(1,'#ffcc33');
      ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.fillRect(tx,t.y+14,t.w,t.h*.55);
      ctx.fillStyle='#332200';ctx.fillRect(tx-6,GY-14,t.w+12,14);
      if(t.hit){ctx.font='20px serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('🔥',tx+t.w*.8,t.y+t.h*.4);}
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.font='bold 14px monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('→',tx+t.w/2,t.y+t.h*.55);
      ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
    }
    else if(t.type==='fly'){
      ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#ff88ff';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?25:8;
      ctx.fillStyle='#441166';ctx.fillRect(tx,t.y,t.w,t.h);
      ctx.fillStyle=t.hit?'#ff88ff':'#cc44ff';ctx.fillRect(tx+2,t.y+2,t.w-4,4);
      for(let b=0;b<4;b++){
        const bx=tx+b*(t.w/3.5),by=t.y+Math.sin(frame*.12+b*1.4)*7;
        ctx.fillStyle=`hsl(${270+b*18},100%,65%)`;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(bx,by,7,4,-.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(bx+10,by,7,4,.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    else if(t.type==='mud'){
      ctx.fillStyle='#3a1500';ctx.fillRect(tx,GY-t.h,t.w,t.h);
      ctx.fillStyle='#5a2a0a';
      for(let mx=0;mx<t.w;mx+=12)ctx.fillRect(tx+mx+2,GY-t.h+3,7,6);
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff5500';ctx.font='9px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='left';
      ctx.fillText('⚠ BOUE ⚠',tx+4,GY-t.h-6);
    }
  }
}

function drawLauncher(){
  const lx=PIV_X-camX,ly=GY,pivX=lx,pivY=ly-PIV_Y_OFF;
  const a=getArmAngle();
  const ex=pivX+Math.cos(a)*ARM_L,ey=pivY+Math.sin(a)*ARM_L;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=20;
  ctx.strokeStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.fillStyle='#000d1a';
  ctx.beginPath();roundRect(ctx,lx-42,ly-78,84,78,7);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#001830';ctx.fillRect(lx-28,ly-72,56,32);
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 6px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK FM',lx,ly-59);
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='5px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.fillText('LAUNCHER',lx,ly-50);
  ['#ff4488','#00ff88','#ffe600'].forEach((col,i)=>{ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(lx-16+i*16,ly-22,5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();});
  ctx.strokeStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.lineWidth=6;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.shadowBlur=25;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pivX,pivY);ctx.lineTo(ex,ey);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=30;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex,ey,11,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(pivX,pivY,6,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawCube(x,y,sz,rot,col,acc){
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rot);
  ctx.shadowColor=col;ctx.shadowBlur=18;
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillRect(-sz,-sz,sz*2,sz*2);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.22)';ctx.fillRect(-sz,-sz,sz*2,sz*.8);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.25)';ctx.fillRect(-sz,sz*.2,sz*2,sz*.8);
  ctx.fillStyle=acc;ctx.fillRect(-sz*.6,-sz*.6,sz*1.2,sz*1.2);
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillRect(-sz*.45,-sz*.45,sz*.9,sz*.9);
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.5)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.strokeRect(-sz,-sz,sz*2,sz*2);
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawCharacter(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar],sz=c.size;
  if(state==='menu'||state==='ready'||state==='charging'){
    const {x,y}=getCubePos();
    drawCube(x,y,sz,state==='charging'?Math.sin(frame*.15)*.08:0,c.col,c.acc);
  } else if(state==='flying'||state==='dead'){
    drawCube(ch.x-camX,ch.y,sz,ch.rot,c.col,c.acc);
  }
}

function drawParticles(){
  for(const p of particles){
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=p.life;ctx.shadowColor=p.col;ctx.shadowBlur=9;
    ctx.fillStyle=p.col;ctx.fillRect(p.x-camX-p.s/2,p.y-p.s/2,p.s,p.s);ctx.restore();
  }
}

function drawGauge(){
  if(state!=='charging')return;
  const gH=200,gW=20,gx=32,gy=H/2-gH/2,pct=chargeValue;
  const good=pct>=.40&&pct<=.76,ok=(pct>=.30&&pct<.40)||(pct>.76&&pct<=.86);
  const col=good?'#00ff88':ok?'#ffe600':'#ff4444';
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,255,136,.10)';ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH*.24,gW,gH*.52);
  ctx.fillStyle='#0a0a0a';ctx.strokeStyle='#333';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.fillRect(gx,gy,gW,gH);ctx.strokeRect(gx,gy,gW,gH);
  const fh=gH*pct;
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,gy+gH,0,gy+gH-fh);
  g.addColorStop(0,col);g.addColorStop(1,col+'44');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH-fh,gW,fh);
  if(good){ctx.shadowColor='#00ff88';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,255,136,.25)';ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH-fh,gW,fh);}
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#888';ctx.font='8px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('POWER',gx+gW/2,gy-10);
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.font='9px Share Tech Mono,monospace';
  ctx.fillText(Math.round(pct*100)+'%',gx+gW+10,gy+gH-fh+4);
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawHUD(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=14;ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 18px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='right';
  ctx.fillText(score+' m',W-24,44);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.fillStyle='#555';ctx.font='12px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.fillText('RECORD: '+best+' m',W-24,62);
  ctx.fillStyle=c.col;ctx.shadowColor=c.col;ctx.shadowBlur=10;ctx.font='bold 12px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='left';
  ctx.fillText(c.name,24,44);
  if(state==='flying'){
    const canDive=diveCooldown<=0;
    ctx.fillStyle=canDive?'#ff4488':'#444';ctx.shadowColor=canDive?'#ff4488':'transparent';ctx.shadowBlur=canDive?14:0;
    ctx.font='10px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='left';
    ctx.fillText(canDive?'[CLIC/ESPACE] → PIQUÉ ▼':'PIQUÉ — cooldown '+Math.ceil(diveCooldown/60)+'s',24,66);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function statBar(x,y,w,h,v,col){
  ctx.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w*Math.min(v,1),h);
}

function drawMenu(){
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=28;ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 24px Press Start 2P,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK LAUNCH',W/2,68);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='11px Press Start 2P,monospace';ctx.fillText('EASTER EGG #18',W/2,96);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.fillStyle='#666';ctx.font='11px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('← → choisir  ·  CLIC ou ESPACE pour commencer',W/2,122);

  const cW=128,cH=148,gap=12,tot=CHARS.length*(cW+gap)-gap;
  const sx=W/2-tot/2,cy=H/2-cH/2-20;
  CHARS.forEach((c,i)=>{
    const cx=sx+i*(cW+gap),sel=i===selChar,lk=c.locked;
    ctx.save();
    if(sel){ctx.shadowColor=c.col;ctx.shadowBlur=30;}
    ctx.fillStyle=sel?c.col+'1a':'#0a0a0a';
    ctx.strokeStyle=sel?c.col:'#252525';ctx.lineWidth=sel?2:1;
    ctx.beginPath();roundRect(ctx,cx,cy,cW,cH,9);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    if(lk){ctx.fillStyle='#333';ctx.font='28px serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('🔒',cx+cW/2,cy+56);}
    else drawCube(cx+cW/2,cy+52,c.size*.9,0,c.col,c.acc);
    ctx.fillStyle=lk?'#3a3a3a':(sel?c.col:'#777');
    ctx.font=`${sel?'8':'7'}px Press Start 2P,monospace`;ctx.textAlign='center';
    c.name.split(' ').forEach((ln,li)=>ctx.fillText(ln,cx+cW/2,cy+84+li*13));
    if(!lk){
      ctx.font='7px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.fillStyle='#555';
      ctx.fillText('MASSE',cx+cW/2-28,cy+108);ctx.fillText('AÉRO',cx+cW/2,cy+108);ctx.fillText('REBOND',cx+cW/2+28,cy+108);
      const bw=(cW-16)/3-2;
      statBar(cx+8,cy+112,bw,5,c.mass/1.8,'#ff4488');
      statBar(cx+8+bw+2,cy+112,bw,5,c.aero/1.4,'#00f0ff');
      statBar(cx+8+(bw+2)*2,cy+112,bw,5,c.elas/.85,'#00ff88');
    }else{ctx.fillStyle='#444';ctx.font='7px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(c.desc,cx+cW/2,cy+120);}
  });
  ctx.textAlign='left';
  if(best>0){ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#ffe600';ctx.shadowColor='#ffe600';ctx.shadowBlur=12;ctx.font='bold 13px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('🏆  RECORD : '+best+' m',W/2,H/2+cH/2+50);ctx.restore();}
  ctx.textAlign='left';
  // Instructions
  const lines=['① CLIC ou ESPACE → armer la catapulte','② Relâcher → lancer le cube','③ CLIC ou ESPACE en vol → PIQUÉ (cooldown 5s)','   Champignon + piqué = super rebond !'];
  ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.55)';ctx.fillRect(W/2-240,H-128,480,108);
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,240,255,.25)';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(W/2-240,H-128,480,108);
  ctx.fillStyle='#777';ctx.font='9px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  lines.forEach((l,i)=>ctx.fillText(l,W/2,H-108+i*17));
  ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
}

function drawDead(){
  const fade=Math.min(1,(140-deathTimer)/35);
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=fade*.78;ctx.fillStyle='#000';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.restore();
  if(fade<.45)return;
  const a=Math.min(1,(fade-.45)/.55);
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a;
  ctx.shadowColor='#ff4488';ctx.shadowBlur=40;ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='bold 32px Press Start 2P,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('GAME OVER',W/2,H/2-60);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=22;ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 22px Press Start 2P,monospace';
  ctx.fillText(score+' M',W/2,H/2);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  if(score>0&&score>=best){ctx.fillStyle='#ffe600';ctx.shadowColor='#ffe600';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.font='11px Press Start 2P,monospace';ctx.fillText('★  NOUVEAU RECORD  ★',W/2,H/2+42);ctx.shadowBlur=0;}
  ctx.fillStyle='#555';ctx.font='10px Share Tech Mono,monospace';ctx.fillText('CLIC / ESPACE pour rejouer  ·  ← → pour changer de perso',W/2,H/2+92);
  ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
}

function roundRect(c,x,y,w,h,r){
  c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x+r,y);c.lineTo(x+w-r,y);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+r);
  c.lineTo(x+w,y+h-r);c.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y+h,x+w-r,y+h);
  c.lineTo(x+r,y+h);c.quadraticCurveTo(x,y+h,x,y+h-r);
  c.lineTo(x,y+r);c.quadraticCurveTo(x,y,x+r,y);c.closePath();
}

// LOOP
function loop(){
  if(!gameRunning)return;
  rafId=requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  frame++;
  if(state==='charging'){chargeValue+=.012*chargeDir;if(chargeValue>=1){chargeValue=1;chargeDir=-1;}if(chargeValue<=0){chargeValue=0;chargeDir=1;}}
  if(state==='flying'){physics();tickP();}
  if(state==='dead'){deathTimer--;tickP();if(deathTimer<=0&&score>best)best=score;}
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  drawBg();drawTrails();drawGround();drawTerrain();drawLauncher();drawCharacter();drawParticles();
  if(state==='menu'){drawMenu();}
  else{drawHUD();drawGauge();}
  if(state==='dead')drawDead();
}

// INPUT
function onAction(){
  if(state==='menu'){state='ready';}
  else if(state==='ready'){state='charging';chargeValue=0;chargeDir=1;}
  else if(state==='charging'){doLaunch();}
  else if(state==='flying'){if(diveCooldown<=0){isDiving=true;diveCooldown=300;setTimeout(()=>{isDiving=false;},300);}}
  else if(state==='dead'&&deathTimer<=0){reset();}
}

let _keyRef,_mouseRef,_touchRef2;
function attachInputs(c){
  _keyRef=e=>{
    if(!gameRunning)return;
    if(e.key===' '||e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();onAction();}
    if(state==='menu'||(state==='dead'&&deathTimer<=0)){
      if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){let n=(selChar-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar)n=(n-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;selChar=n;if(state==='dead')reset();}
      if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){let n=(selChar+1)%CHARS.length;while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar)n=(n+1)%CHARS.length;selChar=n;if(state==='dead')reset();}
    }
  };
  _mouseRef=e=>{e.preventDefault();onAction();};
  _touchRef2=e=>{e.preventDefault();onAction();};
  document.addEventListener('keydown',_keyRef);
  c.addEventListener('mousedown',_mouseRef);
  c.addEventListener('touchstart',_touchRef2,{passive:false});
}
function detachInputs(c){
  document.removeEventListener('keydown',_keyRef);
  if(c){c.removeEventListener('mousedown',_mouseRef);c.removeEventListener('touchstart',_touchRef2);}
}

function initGame(){
  canvas=document.getElementById('jfm-game-canvas');
  ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  resize();
  window.addEventListener('resize',resize);
  gameRunning=true;
  reset();
  attachInputs(canvas);
  loop();
}
function stopGame(){
  gameRunning=false;
  if(rafId){cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);rafId=null;}
  const c=document.getElementById('jfm-game-canvas');
  detachInputs(c);
  window.removeEventListener('resize',resize);
}

window.JFM_GAME={openGameOverlay,closeGameOverlay};

})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

==================================================

  padding:48px 64px;text-align:center;max-width:620px;width:92%;
}

<script>
'use strict';

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   KONAMI LOCK
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const SEQ  = ['ArrowUp','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight','b','a'];
const ICONS = ['↑','↑','↓','↓','←','→','←','→','B','A'];
let kidx = 0;
let lockActive = true;

const krow = document.getElementById('krow');
ICONS.forEach((_,i)=>{
  const d=document.createElement('div');
  d.className='kk';d.id='kk'+i;d.textContent=ICONS[i];
  krow.appendChild(d);
});

document.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{
  if(!lockActive) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  const k = ['b','a'].includes(e.key.toLowerCase()) ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key;
  if(k === SEQ[kidx]){
    const el=document.getElementById('kk'+kidx);
    el.classList.add('flash');
    setTimeout(()=>{ el.classList.remove('flash');el.classList.add('done'); },250);
    kidx++;
    if(kidx===SEQ.length){
      document.getElementById('lunlock').style.display='block';
      setTimeout(unlockGame, 1200);
    }
  } else {
    if(kidx>0){
      document.getElementById('lerr').classList.add('show');
      for(let i=0;i<kidx;i++){
        const el=document.getElementById('kk'+i);
        el.className='kk wrong';
        setTimeout(()=>el.className='kk',600);
      }
      setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('lerr').classList.remove('show'),1800);
      kidx=0;
      if(k===SEQ[0]){ document.getElementById('kk0').classList.add('done'); kidx=1; }
    }
  }
});

function unlockGame(){
  lockActive=false;
  const lock=document.getElementById('lock');
  lock.style.transition='opacity 1s';
  lock.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(()=>{ lock.style.display='none'; gameInit(); }, 1000);
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   GAME ENGINE
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
const canvas=document.getElementById('c');
const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
let W,H,GY; // GY = ground Y

function resize(){
  W=canvas.width=window.innerWidth;
  H=canvas.height=window.innerHeight;
  GY=H-90;
}
resize();
window.addEventListener('resize',resize);

/* ── STATE ── */
let state='menu'; // menu|charging|flying|dead
let charge=0;
let selChar=0;
let isDiving=false;
let score=0;
let best=0;
let camX=0;
let frame=0;
let particles=[];
let trails=[];
let terrain=[];
let spaceHeld=false;
let deathTimer=0;
const ch={x:0,y:0,vx:0,vy:0,rot:0,angV:0};

/* ── CHARACTERS ── */
const CHARS=[
  {name:'CTRL PAD', col:'#00f0ff',acc:'#003344',mass:1.0,aero:1.0, elas:.65,locked:false,desc:'Équilibré · Idéal pour débuter'},
  {name:'JOY STICK',col:'#ffe600',acc:'#333300',mass:.7, aero:1.4, elas:.50,locked:false,desc:'Aérodynamique · Conserve la vitesse'},
  {name:'MEGA PAD', col:'#00ff88',acc:'#003322',mass:1.6,aero:.7,  elas:.82,locked:false,desc:'Lourd · Rebonds dévastateurs'},
  {name:'RETRO X',  col:'#ff4488',acc:'#440022',mass:1.2,aero:1.1, elas:.70,locked:false,desc:'Hybride · Trajectoires variées'},
  {name:'HEADSET',  col:'#bb44ff',acc:'#220044',mass:.9, aero:1.25,elas:.72,locked:true, desc:'Débloqué à 500m'},
  {name:'ARCADE',   col:'#ff8800',acc:'#331100',mass:1.3,aero:.9,  elas:.78,locked:true, desc:'Débloqué à 1000m'},
];

/* ── TERRAIN GEN ── */
function genTerrain(sx,n){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const x=sx+i*(180+Math.random()*280);
    const r=Math.random();
    if(r<.28){
      const s=28+Math.random()*22;
      terrain.push({type:'shroom',x,y:GY-s,w:s*1.3,h:s,pow:1+Math.random()*.5,hit:false});
    } else if(r<.46){
      terrain.push({type:'cloud',x:x+Math.random()*120,y:70+Math.random()*(H*.32),w:110+Math.random()*80,h:26,hit:false});
    } else if(r<.60){
      terrain.push({type:'cannon',x,y:GY-55,w:48,h:55,hit:false});
    } else if(r<.73){
      terrain.push({type:'fly',x:x+Math.random()*60,y:GY-(60+Math.random()*90),w:64,h:36,hit:false});
    } else if(r<.83){
      terrain.push({type:'mud',x,y:GY-10,w:75+Math.random()*55,h:14});
    } else {
      terrain.push({type:'shroom',x,y:GY-38,w:44,h:38,pow:1.3,hit:false});
    }
  }
}

/* ── PHYSICS ── */
function physics(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  const grav=.44*c.mass;
  const drag=1-(.002/c.aero);
  ch.vy+=grav;
  ch.vx*=drag;
  if(isDiving) ch.vy+=.65;
  ch.x+=ch.vx; ch.y+=ch.vy;
  ch.rot+=ch.angV; ch.angV*=.98;
  camX+=(ch.x-W*.25-camX)*.07;
  score=Math.max(score,Math.max(0,Math.floor((ch.x-200)/3)));

  // Ground
  if(ch.y>=GY-14){
    ch.y=GY-14;
    const mud=terrain.find(t=>t.type==='mud'&&ch.x>t.x&&ch.x<t.x+t.w);
    if(mud){ch.vx=0;ch.vy=0;spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#774411',18);state='dead';deathTimer=120;return;}
    const bounce=Math.abs(ch.vy)*c.elas*(isDiving?1.65:1);
    ch.vy=-bounce; ch.vx*=.80; ch.angV=(Math.random()-.5)*.32;
    if(bounce>1.2) spawnTrail(ch.x,GY);
    if(bounce<1.4&&Math.abs(ch.vx)<.8){state='dead';deathTimer=120;}
  }

  // Object collisions
  for(const t of terrain){
    if(t.hit) continue;
    const tx=t.x, tw=t.w;
    const inX=ch.x>tx-tw*.3&&ch.x<tx+tw*1.3;
    if(!inX) continue;
    if(t.type==='shroom'&&ch.y>=t.y-6&&ch.y<=t.y+t.h*.5&&ch.vy>0){
      const db=isDiving?2.1:1;
      ch.vy=-Math.abs(ch.vy)*c.elas*t.pow*db*1.55;
      ch.y=t.y-6; ch.angV=(Math.random()-.5)*.55;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff66ee',14);
      t.hit=true; setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,700);
    }
    else if(t.type==='cannon'&&ch.y>=t.y+5&&ch.y<=t.y+t.h-5&&ch.vy>-4){
      ch.vx=Math.max(Math.abs(ch.vx),16)*1.7; ch.vy=-4; ch.y=t.y;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff8800',18);
      t.hit=true; setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,900);
    }
    else if(t.type==='fly'&&ch.y>=t.y-8&&ch.y<=t.y+t.h){
      ch.vx*=.93; ch.vy=-17;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#ff44ff',12);
      t.hit=true; setTimeout(()=>t.hit=false,500);
    }
    else if(t.type==='cloud'&&ch.x>t.x&&ch.x<t.x+tw&&ch.y>=t.y-6&&ch.y<=t.y+t.h+6&&ch.vy>0){
      ch.vy=-Math.abs(ch.vy)*.92; ch.y=t.y-6;
      spawnP(ch.x,ch.y,'#aae0ff',8);
    }
  }

  // Generate ahead
  const maxT=terrain.length?Math.max(...terrain.map(t=>t.x)):0;
  if(ch.x+W>maxT-400) genTerrain(maxT+200,10);

  // Check unlocks
  if(score>=500) CHARS[4].locked=false;
  if(score>=1000) CHARS[5].locked=false;
}

/* ── PARTICLES ── */
function spawnP(x,y,col,n){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)
    particles.push({x,y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*7,vy:-Math.random()*7-1,col,life:1,s:2+Math.random()*4});
}
function spawnTrail(x,y){trails.push({x,y:y-4,life:1});}
function tickP(){
  for(let i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const p=particles[i];p.x+=p.vx;p.y+=p.vy;p.vy+=.22;p.life-=.028;
    if(p.life<=0)particles.splice(i,1);
  }
  for(let i=trails.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    trails[i].life-=.004;
    if(trails[i].life<=0)trails.splice(i,1);
  }
}

/* ── LAUNCH ── */
function getLaunchAngle(pct){return Math.PI*.44-pct*Math.PI*.36;}
function launch(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  const pct=charge/100;
  const ang=getLaunchAngle(pct);
  const spd=8+pct*19;
  const pivX=150, pivY=GY-58, armL=58;
  const armA=-Math.PI/2+pct*Math.PI*.8;
  ch.x=pivX+Math.cos(armA)*armL;
  ch.y=pivY+Math.sin(armA)*armL;
  ch.vx=Math.cos(-ang)*spd/c.mass;
  ch.vy=-Math.sin(-ang)*spd/c.mass;
  ch.rot=0; ch.angV=(Math.random()-.5)*.3;
  score=0; state='flying';
}

/* ── RESET ── */
function reset(){
  charge=0;isDiving=false;score=0;camX=0;
  particles=[];trails=[];terrain=[];
  ch.x=200;ch.y=GY-80;ch.vx=0;ch.vy=0;ch.rot=0;ch.angV=0;
  genTerrain(300,35);
  state='menu';
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DRAWING
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

/* Background */
function drawBg(){
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#050010');g.addColorStop(.65,'#080020');g.addColorStop(1,'#0d0028');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  // Stars
  ctx.save();
  for(let i=0;i<90;i++){
    const sx=((i*137.508)%W),sy=((i*97.33)%(H*.55));
    const br=(.5+.5*Math.sin(frame*.02+i*.8))*.8;
    ctx.globalAlpha=br;ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,i%4===0?2:1,i%4===0?2:1);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.restore();

  // Neon perspective grid
  const gs=90,ox=(camX*.28)%gs;
  ctx.save();
  // Horizontal lines (perspective rows)
  for(let r=0;r<7;r++){
    const y=GY-r*35;
    const a=.05+r*.04;
    ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(100,0,200,${a})`;ctx.lineWidth=1;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(W,y);ctx.stroke();
  }
  // Vanishing vertical lines
  for(let x=-ox;x<W+gs;x+=gs){
    const prog=(x/W);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(50,0,120,.25)';ctx.lineWidth=1;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,GY-220);ctx.lineTo(x-50,GY);ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();

  // Distant synthwave city
  const cOff=camX*.14;
  for(let i=0;i<22;i++){
    const bx=((i*179+40)%1400-cOff%1400+1400)%1400-120;
    const bh=35+(i*53)%90,bw=18+(i*29)%38;
    ctx.fillStyle=i%2===0?'rgba(0,60,150,.55)':'rgba(60,0,110,.55)';
    ctx.fillRect(bx,GY-195-bh,bw,bh);
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,0,.35)';
    for(let wy=bh-8;wy>8;wy-=11){
      if((i+wy)%3!==0) continue;
      for(let wx=3;wx<bw-3;wx+=7) ctx.fillRect(bx+wx,GY-195-bh+wy,4,5);
    }
  }
}

/* Ground */
function drawGround(){
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,GY-8,0,H);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#1a0055');g.addColorStop(.04,'#0d0033');g.addColorStop(1,'#060018');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,GY-8,W,H-GY+8);
  ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#9900ff';ctx.shadowBlur=14;
  ctx.fillStyle='#bb55ff';ctx.fillRect(0,GY-3,W,3);
  ctx.restore();
}

/* Trails (pixel stars) */
function drawTrails(){
  for(const t of trails){
    const cx=t.x-camX;
    ctx.globalAlpha=t.life*.85;
    const hue=(t.x*.5)%360;
    ctx.fillStyle=`hsl(${hue},100%,70%)`;
    // Small pixel star
    ctx.fillRect(cx-3,t.y,6,2);ctx.fillRect(cx-1,t.y-3,2,8);
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(cx-1,t.y-1,2,2);
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
}

/* Terrain objects */
function drawTerrain(){
  for(const t of terrain){
    const tx=t.x-camX;
    if(tx<-250||tx>W+250) continue;

    if(t.type==='shroom'){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor='#ff44ee';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?35:10;
      // Cap
      ctx.fillStyle=t.hit?'#fff':'#ee66dd';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(tx+t.w*.05,t.y,t.w*.62,t.h*.65,0,Math.PI,0);ctx.fill();
      // Spots
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.05-9,t.y-t.h*.28,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.05+7,t.y-t.h*.18,3,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx+t.w*.05-2,t.y-t.h*.5,2.5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
      // Stem
      ctx.fillStyle='#ccaacc';
      ctx.fillRect(tx+t.w*.05-7,t.y,14,t.h*.45);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    else if(t.type==='cloud'){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor='#88ccff';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?22:10;
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(140,195,255,.72)';
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(tx,t.y,t.w,t.h,13);ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(210,235,255,.5)';
      ctx.fillRect(tx+8,t.y+4,t.w-16,5);
      // FM symbol
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.4)';
      ctx.font='11px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('~FM~',tx+t.w/2,t.y+t.h/2+4);
      ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
    }

    else if(t.type==='cannon'){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor='#ff8800';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?30:12;
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(tx,0,tx+t.w,0);
      g.addColorStop(0,'#221100');g.addColorStop(.4,'#ff7700');g.addColorStop(1,'#ffcc33');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(tx,t.y+12,t.w,[0,t.w/2,t.w/2,0]);ctx.fill();
      ctx.strokeStyle='#ff8800';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.stroke();
      // Base
      ctx.fillStyle='#221100';ctx.fillRect(tx-4,t.y+t.h-12,t.w+8,12);
      // Arrow
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.font='bold 15px monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('→',tx+t.w/2,t.y+t.h-2);
      ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
    }

    else if(t.type==='fly'){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor='#ff88ff';ctx.shadowBlur=t.hit?22:7;
      for(let b=0;b<5;b++){
        const bx=tx+b*13,by=t.y+Math.sin(frame*.12+b*1.3)*9;
        const hue=260+b*15;
        ctx.fillStyle=`hsl(${hue},100%,65%)`;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(bx-5,by,5.5,3,-.45,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(bx+5,by,5.5,3,.45,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle='#222';ctx.fillRect(bx-1,by-1,2,2);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    else if(t.type==='mud'){
      ctx.fillStyle='#3a1500';ctx.fillRect(tx,t.y,t.w,t.h);
      ctx.fillStyle='#5a2a0a';
      for(let mx=0;mx<t.w;mx+=10) ctx.fillRect(tx+mx+2,t.y+2,6,5);
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff3300';ctx.font='9px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='left';
      ctx.fillText('⚠ BOUE ⚠',tx+4,t.y-5);
    }
  }
}

/* Launcher */
function drawLauncher(){
  const lx=150-camX,ly=GY;
  const pivX=lx,pivY=ly-60,armL=60;
  let armA;
  if(state==='menu') armA=-Math.PI/2;
  else if(state==='charging') armA=-Math.PI/2+(charge/100)*Math.PI*.82;
  else armA=-Math.PI*.88;
  const ex=pivX+Math.cos(armA)*armL,ey=pivY+Math.sin(armA)*armL;

  ctx.save();
  // Cabinet base
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=18;
  ctx.strokeStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.lineWidth=2;
  ctx.fillStyle='#000d1a';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(lx-38,ly-68,76,68,6);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
  // Screen
  ctx.fillStyle='#001830';ctx.fillRect(lx-24,ly-62,48,28);
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 7px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK FM',lx,ly-50);
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='7px Share Tech Mono';
  ctx.fillText('LAUNCHER',lx,ly-40);
  // Buttons
  ['#ff4488','#00ff88','#ffe600'].forEach((col,i)=>{
    ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(lx-14+i*14,ly-18,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  });
  // Arm
  ctx.strokeStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.lineCap='round';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pivX,pivY);ctx.lineTo(ex,ey);ctx.stroke();
  // Cup
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=25;
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ex,ey,9,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  // Pivot
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(pivX,pivY,5,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
}

/* Controller character */
function drawCtrl(x,y,sz,rot,col,isLocked){
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rot);
  if(isLocked){ctx.globalAlpha=.4;}
  ctx.shadowColor=col;ctx.shadowBlur=14;
  // Body
  ctx.fillStyle=col;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(-sz,-sz*.55,sz*2,sz*1.1,sz*.38);ctx.fill();
  // Grip nubs
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(-sz+2,sz*.3,sz*.55,sz*.4,sz*.2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(sz*.43,sz*.3,sz*.55,sz*.4,sz*.2);ctx.fill();
  // D-pad
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.5)';
  ctx.fillRect(-sz*.78,-sz*.08,sz*.28,sz*.78);
  ctx.fillRect(-sz*.94,sz*.22,sz*.6,sz*.28);
  // Buttons
  [['#ff4444',.62,-.08],['#ffee00',.78,.18],['#44ff44',.46,.36],['#00aaff',.62,.42]].forEach(([c,bx,by])=>{
    ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(sz*bx-sz,sz*by,sz*.115,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  });
  // Analog sticks
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.4)';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-sz*.35,sz*.38,sz*.14,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sz*.2,sz*.38,sz*.14,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle=col+'99';
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-sz*.35,sz*.38,sz*.09,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sz*.2,sz*.38,sz*.09,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}

/* Character on screen */
function drawCharacter(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  if(state==='menu'||state==='charging'){
    const pivX=150-camX,pivY=GY-60,armL=60;
    let armA=state==='menu'?-Math.PI/2:-Math.PI/2+(charge/100)*Math.PI*.82;
    const cx=pivX+Math.cos(armA)*armL,cy=pivY+Math.sin(armA)*armL;
    drawCtrl(cx,cy,15,0,c.col,false);
  } else {
    drawCtrl(ch.x-camX,ch.y,15,ch.rot,c.col,false);
  }
}

/* Particles */
function drawParticles(){
  for(const p of particles){
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=p.life;
    ctx.shadowColor=p.col;ctx.shadowBlur=7;
    ctx.fillStyle=p.col;
    ctx.fillRect(p.x-camX-p.s/2,p.y-p.s/2,p.s,p.s);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* ── HUD ── */
function drawHUD(){
  const c=CHARS[selChar];
  // Score top right
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=12;
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 16px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='right';
  ctx.fillText(`${score} m`,W-20,36);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.fillStyle='#555';ctx.font='11px Share Tech Mono';
  ctx.fillText(`RECORD: ${best} m`,W-20,54);
  ctx.restore();
  // Character name top left
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle=c.col;ctx.shadowColor=c.col;ctx.shadowBlur=8;
  ctx.font='bold 11px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='left';
  ctx.fillText(c.name,20,36);
  ctx.restore();
  // Dive hint
  if(state==='flying'){
    ctx.save();
    const active=spaceHeld||isDiving;
    ctx.fillStyle=active?'#ff4488':'#444';
    ctx.shadowColor='#ff4488';ctx.shadowBlur=active?14:0;
    ctx.font='11px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='left';
    ctx.fillText(`[ESPACE/CLIC] PIQUÉ ${active?'▼▼▼':''}`,20,56);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

/* Charge gauge */
function drawGauge(){
  if(state!=='charging') return;
  const gH=190,gW=18,gx=28,gy=H/2-gH/2;
  const pct=charge/100;
  const good=pct>=.42&&pct<=.78;
  const ok=(pct>=.32&&pct<.42)||(pct>.78&&pct<=.88);
  const col=good?'#00ff88':ok?'#ffe600':'#ff4444';
  ctx.save();
  // Optimal zone
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,255,136,.12)';ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH*.22,gW,gH*.56);
  // BG
  ctx.fillStyle='#0a0a0a';ctx.strokeStyle='#333';ctx.lineWidth=1;
  ctx.fillRect(gx,gy,gW,gH);ctx.strokeRect(gx,gy,gW,gH);
  // Fill
  const fh=gH*pct;
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,gy+gH,0,gy+gH-fh);
  g.addColorStop(0,col);g.addColorStop(1,col+'44');
  ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH-fh,gW,fh);
  // Glow pulse on optimal
  if(good){ctx.shadowColor='#00ff88';ctx.shadowBlur=15;ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,255,136,.3)';ctx.fillRect(gx,gy+gH-fh,gW,fh);}
  ctx.restore();
  // Labels
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle='#888';ctx.font='9px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('POWER',gx+gW/2,gy-8);
  const ang=getLaunchAngle(pct)*180/Math.PI;
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.font='10px Share Tech Mono';
  ctx.fillText(`${Math.round(ang)}°`,gx+gW+8,gy+gH-fh+4);
  ctx.restore();
}

/* Menu screen */
function drawMenu(){
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=25;
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 22px Press Start 2P';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK LAUNCH',W/2,64);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='11px Press Start 2P';
  ctx.fillText('EASTER EGG #18',W/2,90);
  ctx.restore();

  ctx.fillStyle='#666';ctx.font='12px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('← → pour choisir ton personnage  ·  ESPACE / CLIC pour charger',W/2,116);

  const cW=118,cH=132,gap=14;
  const tot=CHARS.length*(cW+gap)-gap;
  const sx=W/2-tot/2,cy=H/2-cH/2-10;

  CHARS.forEach((c,i)=>{
    const cx=sx+i*(cW+gap),sel=i===selChar,lk=c.locked;
    ctx.save();
    if(sel){ctx.shadowColor=c.col;ctx.shadowBlur=28;}
    ctx.fillStyle=sel?c.col+'18':'#0a0a0a';
    ctx.strokeStyle=sel?c.col:'#252525';ctx.lineWidth=sel?2:1;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(cx,cy,cW,cH,8);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    if(lk){
      ctx.fillStyle='#333';ctx.font='28px monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('🔒',cx+cW/2,cy+52);
    } else {
      drawCtrl(cx+cW/2,cy+50,16,0,c.col,false);
    }
    ctx.fillStyle=lk?'#3a3a3a':(sel?c.col:'#777');
    ctx.font=`${sel?'9':'8'}px Press Start 2P`;ctx.textAlign='center';
    const nLines=c.name.split(' ');
    nLines.forEach((ln,li)=>ctx.fillText(ln,cx+cW/2,cy+78+li*12));
    if(!lk){
      statBar(cx+7,cy+96,cW-14,5,c.mass/1.6,'#ff4488');
      statBar(cx+7,cy+106,cW-14,5,c.aero/1.4,'#00f0ff');
      statBar(cx+7,cy+116,cW-14,5,c.elas/.82,'#00ff88');
      ctx.fillStyle='#333';ctx.font='6px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('MASSE  AÉRO  REBOND',cx+cW/2,cy+cH-5);
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle='#444';ctx.font='8px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText(c.desc,cx+cW/2,cy+108);
    }
  });
  ctx.textAlign='left';

  if(best>0){
    ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#ffe600';ctx.shadowColor='#ffe600';ctx.shadowBlur=10;
    ctx.font='bold 13px Share Tech Mono';ctx.textAlign='center';
    ctx.fillText(`🏆  RECORD : ${best} m`,W/2,H/2+cH/2+40);
    ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
  }
}

function statBar(x,y,w,h,v,col){
  ctx.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
  ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w*Math.min(v,1),h);
}

/* Death screen */
function drawDead(){
  const fade=Math.min(1,(120-deathTimer)/30);
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=fade*.75;
  ctx.fillStyle='#000';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  ctx.restore();
  if(fade<.5) return;
  const a=Math.min(1,(fade-.5)/.5);
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a;
  ctx.shadowColor='#ff4488';ctx.shadowBlur=35;
  ctx.fillStyle='#ff4488';ctx.font='bold 28px Press Start 2P';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('GAME OVER',W/2,H/2-55);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  ctx.shadowColor='#00f0ff';ctx.shadowBlur=18;
  ctx.fillStyle='#00f0ff';ctx.font='bold 20px Press Start 2P';
  ctx.fillText(`${score} M`,W/2,H/2);
  ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  if(score>0&&score>=best){
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffe600';ctx.shadowColor='#ffe600';ctx.shadowBlur=15;
    ctx.font='10px Press Start 2P';
    ctx.fillText('★  NOUVEAU RECORD  ★',W/2,H/2+38);
    ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  }
  ctx.fillStyle='#555';ctx.font='10px Share Tech Mono';
  ctx.fillText('ESPACE / CLIC pour rejouer  ·  ← → pour changer de perso',W/2,H/2+85);
  ctx.restore();ctx.textAlign='left';
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MAIN LOOP
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function gameInit(){reset();loop();}

function loop(){
  frame++;
  if(state==='charging'&&spaceHeld) charge=Math.min(charge+1,100);
  if(state==='flying'){physics();tickP();}
  if(state==='dead'){
    deathTimer--;
    if(deathTimer<=0){if(score>best)best=score;}
  }
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  drawBg();drawTrails();drawGround();drawTerrain();
  drawLauncher();drawCharacter();drawParticles();
  if(state==='menu') drawMenu();
  else{drawHUD();drawGauge();}
  if(state==='dead') drawDead();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   INPUT
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function onDown(e){
  if(lockActive) return;
  if(e&&e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
  spaceHeld=true;
  if(state==='menu'){state='charging';charge=0;}
  else if(state==='flying'&&!isDiving) isDiving=true;
  else if(state==='dead'&&deathTimer<=0) reset();
}
function onUp(e){
  if(lockActive) return;
  spaceHeld=false;
  if(state==='charging'){launch();}
  if(state==='flying') isDiving=false;
}
function onKey(e){
  if(lockActive) return;
  if(e.key===' '||e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();onDown(e);}
  if(state==='menu'){
    if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){
      let n=(selChar-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;
      while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar) n=(n-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;
      selChar=n;
    }
    if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){
      let n=(selChar+1)%CHARS.length;
      while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar) n=(n+1)%CHARS.length;
      selChar=n;
    }
  }
}
function onKeyUp(e){
  if(lockActive) return;
  if(e.key===' '||e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();onUp(e);}
  if(state==='dead'&&deathTimer<=0&&(e.key==='ArrowLeft'||e.key==='ArrowRight')){
    reset();
    if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){let n=(selChar-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar)n=(n-1+CHARS.length)%CHARS.length;selChar=n;}
    else{let n=(selChar+1)%CHARS.length;while(CHARS[n].locked&&n!==selChar)n=(n+1)%CHARS.length;selChar=n;}
  }
}

document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey);
document.addEventListener('keyup',onKeyUp);
canvas.addEventListener('mousedown',onDown);
canvas.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
canvas.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{e.preventDefault();onDown(e);},{passive:false});
canvas.addEventListener('touchend',e=>{e.preventDefault();onUp(e);},{passive:false});

// roundRect polyfill
if(!CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.roundRect){
  CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.roundRect=function(x,y,w,h,r){
    const rad=Array.isArray(r)?r[0]:r;
    this.moveTo(x+rad,y);this.lineTo(x+w-rad,y);this.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y,x+w,y+rad);
    this.lineTo(x+w,y+h-rad);this.quadraticCurveTo(x+w,y+h,x+w-rad,y+h);
    this.lineTo(x+rad,y+h);this.quadraticCurveTo(x,y+h,x,y+h-rad);
    this.lineTo(x,y+rad);this.quadraticCurveTo(x,y,x+rad,y);this.closePath();
    return this;
  };
}
</script>
</body>
</html>

    4: { 
      title: "Le pixel art : d'une contrainte technique à un choix artistique", 
      content: `
        <h2 style="margin-bottom:1rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">Faire plus avec moins : la nécessité des origines</h2>
        <p>Dans les années 80 et 90, le pixel art n'était pas un style : c'était une <strong>nécessité absolue</strong>. Les développeurs devaient faire rentrer des univers entiers dans des cartouches de quelques kilo-octets. La NES ne pouvait afficher que 52 couleurs simultanées, avec des sprites limités à 8×8 ou 16×16 pixels. Sur Commodore 64, certains graphistes réalisaient des portraits expressifs avec 16 couleurs fixes et une résolution de 160x200 pixels. Chaque pixel comptait, littéralement.</p>

        <h3 style="margin:1.5rem 0 0.75rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">Les contraintes techniques qui ont façonné l'esthétique</h3>
        <p>Comprendre le pixel art, c'est comprendre les contraintes hardware qui l'ont engendré. Les programmeurs de l'époque avaient développé des techniques ingénieuses pour contourner les limitations :</p>
        <ul style="margin-left:1.5rem;color:var(--texte);line-height:2.2;">
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">Le dithering</strong> — Alterner deux couleurs en damier pour simuler une troisième teinte. Utilisé massivement sur GameBoy, dont la palette ne comptait que 4 nuances de vert.</li>
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">L'antialiasing manuel</strong> — Placer manuellement des pixels intermédiaires sur les contours pour adoucir les "escaliers" (jaggies).</li>
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">L'animation économique</strong> — Bouger 3 ou 4 frames pour simuler une animation fluide (le walk cycle de Mario Bros. ne compte que 3 frames).</li>
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">Les palettes de couleurs partagées</strong> — Sur NES, chaque tuile de 8x8 pixels ne pouvait utiliser que 4 couleurs d'une sous-palette. Les graphistes organisaient leur niveau entier autour de cette contrainte.</li>
        </ul>
        <img src='${BASE}/assets/images/blog/pixel-art/contraintes.png' alt='Contraintes techniques du pixel art'>

        <br><h3 style="margin:1.5rem 0 0.75rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">L'imagination au pouvoir</h3>
        <p>Le pixel art fonctionne comme l'impressionnisme. Il ne montre pas les détails, il les <em>suggère</em>. Un sprite de 16x16 pixels représentant un héros demande au cerveau du joueur de faire la moitié du travail pour "compléter" l'image — exactement comme notre cerveau perçoit un visage dans une tache de café. Cette complétion cognitive crée une <strong>implication émotionnelle plus forte</strong> qu'un modèle 3D hyperréaliste où tout est donné clé en main.</p>
        <p>C'est le paradoxe fascinant du pixel art : en montrant moins, il évoque davantage.</p>
        <img src='${BASE}/assets/images/blog/pixel-art/imagination.png' alt='L\'imagination au pouvoir du pixel art'>

        <br><h3 style="margin:1.5rem 0 0.75rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">Le renouveau indépendant : un choix esthétique revendiqué</h3>
        <p>Aujourd'hui, alors que l'Unreal Engine 5 permet de créer des environnements photoréalistes en 4K avec du ray-tracing en temps réel, pourquoi la scène indépendante continue-t-elle de plébisciter le pixel art ? La réponse tient en trois raisons :</p>
        <ol style="margin-left:1.5rem;color:var(--texte);line-height:2.2;">
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">La nostalgie consciente</strong> — Pour les joueurs de 25-40 ans, le pixel art <em>est</em> le jeu vidéo. Stardew Valley (Eric Barone, développé seul) a vendu plus de 20 millions d'exemplaires précisément parce que son esthétique SNES déclenche une madeleine de Proust chez toute une génération.</li>
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">L'accessibilité de création</strong> — Un développeur solo peut maîtriser le pixel art avec des outils comme <strong>Aseprite</strong>, <strong>Piskel</strong> ou même GraphicsGale. Créer un sprite 32x32 de qualité demande des heures, pas des années d'apprentissage de la 3D.</li>
          <li><strong style="color:var(--bleu-neon);">Une identité visuelle forte</strong> — Dans un marché saturé de jeux hyperréalistes, le pixel art se distingue immédiatement. Dead Cells, Celeste, Undertale, Shovel Knight, Blasphemous — ces titres sont reconnaissables en un coup d'œil.</li>
        </ol>
        <img src='${BASE}/assets/images/blog/pixel-art/independant.jpg' alt='Le pixel art dans les jeux indépendants'>

        <br><h3 style="margin:1.5rem 0 0.75rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">Les outils du pixel artiste moderne</h3>
        <p><strong>Aseprite</strong> est devenu la référence absolue : gestion des animations frame par frame, palettes customisables, onion-skinning (voir les frames précédentes en transparence), export automatique des spritesheets. Disponible à moins de 20€, son code source est même open-source. Pour les débutants, <strong>Piskel</strong> (gratuit, en ligne) permet de se lancer sans installation. Et pour les puristes qui veulent recréer les contraintes NES exactes, des outils comme <strong>NES Screen Tool</strong> forcent le respect des limitations hardware d'origine.</p>
        <img src='${BASE}/assets/images/blog/pixel-art/outils.jpg' alt='Outils de création de pixel art'>
        <br><p style="margin-top:1.5rem;font-style:italic;color:var(--texte-dim);">Vous faites du pixel art ? Envoyer vos créations aux crew !</p>
      `
    },

Il connaît chaque donjon par cœur avant d'y mettre les pieds. D'un côté, c'est pratique dans un groupe (il vous guide). De l'autre, il gâche un peu la magie de la découverte. Sa phrase fétiche : "Attends, le boss drop le set légendaire uniquement le mardi entre 18h et 20h si Jupiter est en opposition avec Mars."</p>

        <p>Puisque le marché change à une vitesse folle, l'équipe de JoyStick FM a sorti sa boule de cristal pour des prédictions sur le gaming en 2034. Disclaimer : aucune responsabilité si ces prévisions se révèlent exactes. Ou inexactes. Bref, aucune responsabilité.</p>
        
        <br><h3 style="margin:1.5rem 0 0.75rem;color:var(--bleu-neon);">1. GTA VI sortira enfin sur PC</h3>
        <p>Blague à part, la durée de développement des jeux "Quadruple A" va devenir <strong>proprement insoutenable</strong>. Des jeux comme Elder Scrolls 6 (annoncé en 2018, toujours pas sorti en 2026) ou The Witcher 4 prendront tellement de temps que les studios ne sortiront plus qu'un seul titre par décennie. Les budgets dépasseront le milliard de dollars pour les productions les plus ambitieuses. Et ironiquement, un RPG indépendant à 20€ fait en pixel art par trois personnes dans un garage sera probablement meilleur.</p>
        <img src='${BASE}/assets/images/blog/futur/blague.png' alt='Blague GTA VI'>

Certains prototypes existent déjà en 2026 (Inworld AI, convAI). Ce sera la norme en 2034. La conséquence inattendue : les gens vont tomber amoureux de leurs PNJs.
