/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RESIZE
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function resize() {
  if (!canvas) return;
  W = canvas.width  = Math.min(window.innerWidth,  1400);
  H = canvas.height = Math.min(window.innerHeight, 860);
  canvas.style.width  = W + 'px';
  canvas.style.height = H + 'px';
  GY = H - 100;
  SECRET_CANNON.y = GY - SECRET_CANNON.h;
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TERRAIN GENERATION
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function genTerrain(sx, n) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x = sx + i * (200 + Math.random() * 260);
    const r = Math.random();
    if (r < .28) {
      // Gant de boxe (remplace champignon)
      const h = 45 + Math.random() * 15;
      const w = 40;
      terrain.push({ type: 'glove', x, y: GY - h, w, h, pow: 1.4 + Math.random() * .8, hit: false });
    } else if (r < .42) {
      terrain.push({ type: 'cloud', x: x + Math.random() * 100, y: 80 + Math.random() * (H * .28), w: 120 + Math.random() * 80, h: 30, hit: false });
    } else if (r < .56) {
      terrain.push({ type: 'cannon', x, y: GY - 60, w: 54, h: 60, hit: false });
    } else if (r < .68) {
      terrain.push({ type: 'fly', x: x + Math.random() * 60, y: GY - (70 + Math.random() * 100), w: 72, h: 18, hit: false });
    } else if (r < .78) {
      terrain.push({ type: 'mud', x, y: GY - 16, w: 90 + Math.random() * 60, h: 18 });
    } else {
      // Double gant
      const h = 35 + Math.random() * 15;
      terrain.push({ type: 'glove', x, y: GY - h, w, h, pow: 1.8, hit: false });
    }
  }
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   TREBUCHET — PIVOT & ARM
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function getPivY() { return GY - PIV_Y_OFF; }
function getArmTipPos(angle, worldSpace) {
  const px = worldSpace ? PIV_X : PIV_X - camX;
  const py = getPivY();
  return {
    x: px + Math.cos(angle) * ARM_L,
    y: py + Math.sin(angle) * ARM_L
  };
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   LAUNCH — Tangential release from trebuchet
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function doLaunch() {
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  const tip = getArmTipPos(armAngle, true);
  // Vitesse tangentielle = omega * rayon
  const tangentX = -Math.sin(armAngle) * armSpeed * ARM_L;
  const tangentY =  Math.cos(armAngle) * armSpeed * ARM_L;
  const massScale = 1.0 / Math.sqrt(c.mass);
  ch.x = tip.x;
  ch.y = tip.y;
  // Boost de lancement énorme
  ch.vx = tangentX * massScale * 0.9;
  ch.vy = tangentY * massScale * 0.9;
  // Propulser avec grande vitesse vers la droite
  if (ch.vx < 12) ch.vx += 12;
  // Assurer que la trajectoire part bien vers le haut pour voler loin
  if (ch.vy > -8) ch.vy = -8 - Math.abs(ch.vx) * 0.3;
  ch.rot = 0;
  ch.angV = (Math.random() - .5) * .4;
  score = 0;
  canDive = true;
  isDiving = false;
  diveFrames = 0;
  state = 'flying';
  camX = 0;
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PHYSICS — Rigid Body Dynamics (v3.5)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function physics() {
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  const sz = c.size;
  const hw = c.hitW ? c.size * c.hitW : c.size;
  const hh = c.hitH ? c.size * c.hitH : c.size;
  /* ── Cooldown piqué ── */
  if (diveCooldownTimer > 0) {
    diveCooldownTimer--;
    if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) { diveCooldownTimer = 0; canDive = true; }
  }
  /* ── Cannon absorption ── */
  if (isAbsorbed) {
    absorbTimer--;
    if (absorbCannon) {
      ch.x = absorbCannon.x + absorbCannon.w / 2;
      ch.y = absorbCannon.y + absorbCannon.h / 2;
    }
    if (absorbTimer <= 0) {
      isAbsorbed = false;
      const isSecret = absorbCannon && absorbCannon.type === 'secret_cannon';
      if (isSecret) {
        ch.vx = 45; ch.vy = -22; secretFired = true;
        if (absorbCannon) {
          ch.x = absorbCannon.x + absorbCannon.w + hw;
          ch.y = absorbCannon.y - hh;
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff4400', 30);
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ffaa00', 16);
        }
      } else {
        if (absorbCannon) {
          ch.vx = 22; ch.vy = -10;
          ch.x = absorbCannon.x + absorbCannon.w + hw;
          ch.y = absorbCannon.y - hh;
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff8800', 22);
          setTimeout(() => { if (absorbCannon) absorbCannon.hit = false; }, 800);
        }
      }
      absorbCannon = null;
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
    }
    camX += (ch.x - W * .3 - camX) * .08;
    return;
  }
  // Gravity
  ch.vy += GRAVITY * c.mass;
  // Air drag
  const drag = AIR_DRAG + (c.aero - 1) * 0.0005;
  ch.vx *= drag;
  /* ── Piqué — comportement selon diveType ── */
  if (isDiving && diveFrames > 0) {
    const dt = c.diveType || 'vertical';
    if (dt === 'zerograv') {
      ch.vy -= GRAVITY * c.mass; // annule la gravité
      ch.vy += 0.04;             // descente lente contrôlée
    } else if (dt === 'heavy') {
      ch.vy += DIVE_ACCEL * 2.2; // écrasement amplifié
    } else if (dt === 'diagonal') {
      ch.vy += DIVE_ACCEL;
      ch.vx += (ch.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1) * 0.35; // boost horizontal
    } else {
      ch.vy += DIVE_ACCEL;       // vertical : standard
    }
    diveFrames--;
    if (diveFrames <= 0) isDiving = false;
  }
  // Move
  ch.x += ch.vx;
  ch.y += ch.vy;
  ch.rot += ch.angV;
  const airAngDamp = 0.970 + c.mass * 0.006 + c.size * 0.0003;
  ch.angV *= Math.min(0.992, airAngDamp);
  // Camera smooth follow
  camX += (ch.x - W * .3 - camX) * .08;
  // Distance tracking (5 pixels = 1 mètre)
  currentDist = Math.floor((ch.x - 280) / 5.0);
  if (currentDist > maxDist) maxDist = currentDist;
  if (currentDist < minDist) minDist = currentDist;
  score = Math.max(0, maxDist);
  const altM = Math.max(0, Math.round((GY - ch.y) / 5.0));
  if (altM > maxAlt) maxAlt = altM;
  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     COLLISION SOL — RIGID BODY DYNAMICS (Point de Contact)
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     1. Calcul des 4 coins du rectangle tourné
     2. Point de contact P = coin le plus bas
     3. Impulsion normale J_n → rebond + torque
     4. Impulsion tangentielle J_t → friction de Coulomb → culbute
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
  {
    // Moment d'inertie : I = m * (w² + h²) / 12
    const I = c.mass * (4 * hw * hw + 4 * hh * hh) / 12.0;
    // Coins du rectangle tourné
    const cos_r = Math.cos(ch.rot);
    const sin_r = Math.sin(ch.rot);
    const corners_wy = [
       hw * sin_r + hh * cos_r,
      -hw * sin_r + hh * cos_r,
       hw * sin_r - hh * cos_r,
      -hw * sin_r - hh * cos_r,
    ];
    const corners_wx = [
       hw * cos_r - hh * sin_r,
      -hw * cos_r - hh * sin_r,
       hw * cos_r + hh * sin_r,
      -hw * cos_r + hh * sin_r,
    ];
    // Coin le plus bas
    let maxWy = corners_wy[0], bestIdx = 0;
    for (let ci = 1; ci < 4; ci++) {
      if (corners_wy[ci] > maxWy) { maxWy = corners_wy[ci]; bestIdx = ci; }
    }
    const contactWorldY = ch.y + maxWy;
    if (contactWorldY >= GY) {
      // Snap anti-pénétration
      const penetration = contactWorldY - GY;
      ch.y -= penetration;
      const dx = corners_wx[bestIdx];
      const dy = maxWy - penetration;
      // Boue — stoppe immédiatement
      const mud = terrain.find(t =>
        t.type === 'mud' && ch.x + hw > t.x && ch.x - hw < t.x + t.w
      );
      if (mud) {
        ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0; ch.angV = 0;
        spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#774411', 22);
        state = 'dead'; deathTimer = 140;
        return;
      }
      const wasDiving = isDiving;
      isDiving = false; diveFrames = 0;
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
      // Coefficient de restitution
      const e = Math.min(0.96, c.elas * (wasDiving ? 1.30 : 1.0));
      const mu = c.mu || 0.50;
      // Vitesse du point de contact
      const v_py = ch.vy + ch.angV * dx;
      if (v_py > 0) {
        // Impulsion normale (rebond vertical + torque)
        const denom_n = 1.0 / c.mass + (dx * dx) / I;
        const J_n = (1.0 + e) * v_py / denom_n;
        ch.vy   -= J_n / c.mass;
        ch.angV -= dx * J_n / I;
        // Impulsion tangentielle (friction = culbute)
        const v_tang = ch.vx - ch.angV * dy;
        const denom_t = 1.0 / c.mass + (dy * dy) / I;
        const J_t_stop = -v_tang / denom_t;
        const J_t_max = mu * J_n;
        const J_t = Math.max(-J_t_max, Math.min(J_t_max, J_t_stop));
        ch.vx   += J_t / c.mass;
        ch.angV -= dy * J_t / I;
        // Anti-instabilité
        ch.angV = Math.max(-4.5, Math.min(4.5, ch.angV));
        // Particules visuelles
        const pCol = c.col;
        if (wasDiving) {
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff4488', 20);
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, pCol, 14);
          spawnTrail(ch.x, GY);
        } else if (J_n > 6) {
          spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, pCol, Math.min(24, Math.floor(J_n * 0.55)));
          if (J_n > 14) spawnTrail(ch.x, GY);
        }
        bounceCount++;
        // Sleep state : arrêt basé sur énergie cinétique résiduelle (seuil augmenté → plus de rebonds)
        const KE_lin = 0.5 * c.mass * (ch.vx * ch.vx + ch.vy * ch.vy);
        const KE_rot = 0.5 * I * ch.angV * ch.angV;
        if (KE_lin + KE_rot < 1.6) {
          ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0; ch.angV = 0;
          state = 'dead';
          deathTimer = 140;
          flowerPosX.push(ch.x);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // ── Terrain collisions ──
  for (const t of terrain) {
    if (t.hit) continue;
    const inX = ch.x + sz > t.x - 4 && ch.x - sz < t.x + t.w + 4;
    if (!inX) continue;
    if (t.type === 'glove' && ch.x + sz > t.x - 10 && ch.x - sz < t.x + t.w + 10 && ch.y + sz >= t.y - 12 && ch.y - sz <= t.y + t.h) {
      if (!t.hit) {
        const diveMult = isDiving ? 2.5 : 1;
        ch.vy = -Math.max(Math.abs(ch.vy), 12) * c.elas * t.pow * diveMult;
        ch.vx = Math.max(Math.abs(ch.vx) * 1.25, 10);
        ch.y = t.y - sz - 4;
        ch.angV = (Math.random() - .5) * 1.2;
        spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff2222', 20);
        t.hit = true;
        isDiving = false; diveFrames = 0;
        if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
        setTimeout(() => t.hit = false, 500);
      }
    }
    else if (t.type === 'cannon' && ch.x > t.x + 4 && ch.x < t.x + t.w - 4 && ch.y + sz >= t.y + 8 && ch.y <= t.y + t.h) {
      // Absorption dans le canon
      isAbsorbed = true; absorbTimer = 20; absorbCannon = t;
      ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0; ch.angV = 0;
      t.hit = true;
      cannonHitCount++;
      isDiving = false; diveFrames = 0;
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
      spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff8800', 30);
    }
    else if (t.type === 'fly' && ch.y + sz >= t.y && ch.y <= t.y + t.h + 6 && ch.vy > 0) {
      ch.vy = -24;
      ch.vx *= 1.1;
      ch.y = t.y - sz;
      spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff44ff', 14);
      t.hit = true;
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
      setTimeout(() => t.hit = false, 400);
    }
    else if (t.type === 'cloud' && ch.x > t.x && ch.x < t.x + t.w && ch.y + sz >= t.y && ch.y <= t.y + t.h + 8 && ch.vy > 0) {
      ch.vy = -Math.abs(ch.vy) * .88;
      ch.y = t.y - sz;
      spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#aae0ff', 10);
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
    }
  }
  /* ── Canon secret ── */
  if (!secretFired && !isAbsorbed) {
    const sc = SECRET_CANNON;
    if (ch.x > sc.x && ch.x < sc.x + sc.w && ch.y + sz >= sc.y && ch.y <= sc.y + sc.h) {
      isAbsorbed = true; absorbTimer = 30; absorbCannon = sc;
      ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0; ch.angV = 0;
      isDiving = false; diveFrames = 0;
      if (diveCooldownTimer <= 0) canDive = true;
      spawnP(ch.x, ch.y, '#ff4400', 25);
    }
  }
  // -1000m Easter Egg (Youtube popup)
  if (ch.x < -1000 && !window._EGG_1000_TRIGGERED) {
    window._EGG_1000_TRIGGERED = true;
    ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0;
    state = 'dead'; deathTimer = 10000;
    setTimeout(() => {
      const d = document.createElement('div');
      d.innerHTML = `
      <div style="position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:999999;background:#000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-direction:column;">
         <h1 style="color:#f00;font-family:monospace;margin-bottom:20px;">-1000 METRES ATTEINTS !</h1>
         <iframe width="560" height="315" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/dQw4w9WgXcQ?autoplay=1" frameborder="0" allow="autoplay; fullscreen" style="max-width:90%"></iframe>
         <button onclick="this.parentElement.remove(); window._EGG_1000_TRIGGERED=false; window.JFM_GAME.closeGameOverlay();" style="margin-top:20px;padding:10px 20px;background:#f00;color:#fff;border:none;cursor:pointer;font-family:monospace;">FERMER</button>
      </div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(d.firstElementChild);
    }, 500);
  }
  // Generate terrain ahead
  const maxT = terrain.length ? Math.max(...terrain.map(t => t.x)) : 0;
  if (ch.x + W > maxT - 400) genTerrain(maxT + 200, 12);
  // Unlocks
  if (score >= 500) CHARS[4].locked = false;
  if (score >= 1000) CHARS[5].locked = false;
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   PARTICLES
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function spawnP(x, y, col, n) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++)
    particles.push({ x, y, vx: (Math.random() - .5) * 9, vy: -Math.random() * 9 - 1, col, life: 1, s: 3 + Math.random() * 5 });
}
function spawnTrail(x, y) { trails.push({ x, y: y - 4, life: 1 }); }
function tickP() {
  for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const p = particles[i]; p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.vy += .24; p.life -= .025;
    if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
  }
  for (let i = trails.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    trails[i].life -= .003;
    if (trails[i].life <= 0) trails.splice(i, 1);
  }
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   RESET
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function reset() {
  armAngle = ARM_REST;
  armSpeed = 0;
  isDiving = false;
  diveFrames = 0;
  canDive = true;
  diveCooldownTimer = 0;
  diveUsedCount = 0;
  score = 0;
  currentDist = 0;
  maxDist = 0;
  minDist = 0;
  maxAlt = 0;
  camX = 0;
  particles = [];
  trails = [];
  terrain = [];
  flowerPosX = [];
  bounceCount = 0;
  cannonHitCount = 0;
  secretFired = false;
  SECRET_CANNON.hit = false;
  isAbsorbed = false;
  absorbTimer = 0;
  absorbCannon = null;
  ch.x = PIV_X; ch.y = getPivY() - ARM_L - CHARS[selChar].size;
  ch.vx = 0; ch.vy = 0; ch.rot = 0; ch.angV = 0;
  genTerrain(350, 40);
  state = 'menu';
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DRAWING
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawBg() {
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#03000f');
  g.addColorStop(.6, '#060020');
  g.addColorStop(1, '#0a0030');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  // Stars
  ctx.save();
  for (let i = 0; i < 110; i++) {
    const sx = ((i * 137.508) % W), sy = ((i * 97.33) % (H * .5));
    const br = (.4 + .6 * Math.sin(frame * .018 + i * .9)) * .9;
    ctx.globalAlpha = br;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(sx, sy, i % 5 === 0 ? 2 : 1, i % 5 === 0 ? 2 : 1);
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  ctx.restore();
  // Perspective grid
  const gs = 100, ox = (camX * .25) % gs;
  ctx.save();
  for (let r = 0; r < 8; r++) {
    const y = GY - r * 30;
    const a = .04 + r * .035;
    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(100,0,220,${a})`; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke();
  }
  for (let x = -ox; x < W + gs; x += gs) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,0,130,.2)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, GY - 240); ctx.lineTo(x - 55, GY); ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  // City silhouette
  const cOff = camX * .12;
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    const bx = ((i * 179 + 40) % 1600 - cOff % 1600 + 1600) % 1600 - 120;
    const bh = 40 + (i * 53) % 100, bw = 20 + (i * 31) % 42;
    ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? 'rgba(0,50,140,.5)' : 'rgba(50,0,100,.5)';
    ctx.fillRect(bx, GY - 200 - bh, bw, bh);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,220,0,.3)';
    for (let wy = bh - 8; wy > 8; wy -= 12) {
      if ((i + wy) % 3 !== 0) continue;
      for (let wx = 3; wx < bw - 3; wx += 8) ctx.fillRect(bx + wx, GY - 200 - bh + wy, 4, 6);
    }
  }
}
function drawGround() {
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, GY - 6, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, '#1a0060');
  g.addColorStop(.05, '#0d0038');
  g.addColorStop(1, '#04000f');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, GY - 6, W, H - GY + 6);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor = '#9900ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#cc66ff';
  ctx.fillRect(0, GY - 3, W, 3);
  ctx.restore();
  for (const fx of flowerPosX) {
    const sx = fx - camX;
    if (sx < -60 || sx > W + 60) continue;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.font = '18px serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('\uD83C\uDF38', sx, GY - 2);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
function drawTrails() {
  for (const t of trails) {
    const cx = t.x - camX;
    ctx.globalAlpha = t.life * .9;
    const hue = (t.x * .4) % 360;
    ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${hue},100%,70%)`;
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 4, t.y, 8, 2);
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 1, t.y - 4, 2, 10);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.fillRect(cx - 1, t.y - 1, 2, 2);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}
function drawTerrain() {
  /* ── Canon secret ── */
  if (!secretFired) {
    const sc = SECRET_CANNON;
    const scx = sc.x - camX;
    if (scx > -200 && scx < W + 200) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff4400'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(scx, sc.y, scx + sc.w, sc.y);
      grad.addColorStop(0, '#220000'); grad.addColorStop(.5, '#cc2200'); grad.addColorStop(1, '#ff6600');
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(scx, sc.y, sc.w, sc.h * .55, [0, sc.w * .4, sc.w * .4, 0]);
      else ctx.rect(scx, sc.y, sc.w, sc.h * .55);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#440000'; ctx.fillRect(scx - 6, GY - 14, sc.w + 12, 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = 'bold 10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('\u2620 SECRET', scx + sc.w / 2, sc.y + sc.h * .35);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4400'; ctx.font = '20px serif';
      ctx.fillText('\uD83D\uDCA3', scx + sc.w * .8, sc.y - 5);
      ctx.restore();
      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    }
  }
  /* ── Terrain normal ── */
  for (const t of terrain) {
    const tx = t.x - camX;
    if (tx < -300 || tx > W + 300) continue;
    if (t.type === 'glove') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff2222';
      ctx.shadowBlur = t.hit ? 30 : 10;
      // Tige / Ressort
      ctx.fillStyle = '#666';
      const ext = t.hit ? 15 : 0;
      ctx.fillRect(tx + t.w / 2 - 4, GY - t.h / 2, 8, t.h / 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#aaa';
      ctx.fillRect(tx + t.w / 2 - 6, GY - t.h / 2 + 5, 12, 3);
      ctx.fillRect(tx + t.w / 2 - 6, GY - t.h / 2 + 15, 12, 3);
      
      // Gant
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ee1111';
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(tx - ext, GY - t.h - ext, t.w, t.h * 0.7, 10);
      else ctx.rect(tx - ext, GY - t.h - ext, t.w, t.h * 0.7);
      ctx.fill();
      // Detail
      ctx.fillStyle = '#aa0000';
      ctx.fillRect(tx + 5 - ext, GY - t.h + 10 - ext, t.w - 10, 5);
      
      if (t.hit) {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        ctx.font = 'bold 16px sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText('POW!', tx - 10, GY - t.h - 15);
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    else if (t.type === 'cloud') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#88ccff'; ctx.shadowBlur = t.hit ? 25 : 10;
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(140,195,255,.75)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(tx, t.y, t.w, t.h, 15);
      else { ctx.moveTo(tx + 15, t.y); ctx.lineTo(tx + t.w - 15, t.y); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + t.w, t.y, tx + t.w, t.y + 15); ctx.lineTo(tx + t.w, t.y + t.h - 15); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(tx + t.w, t.y + t.h, tx + t.w - 15, t.y + t.h); ctx.lineTo(tx + 15, t.y + t.h); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, t.y + t.h, tx, t.y + t.h - 15); ctx.lineTo(tx, t.y + 15); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(tx, t.y, tx + 15, t.y); ctx.closePath(); }
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.45)';
      ctx.font = '10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('~FM~', tx + t.w / 2, t.y + t.h / 2 + 4);
      ctx.restore(); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    }
    else if (t.type === 'cannon') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff8800'; ctx.shadowBlur = t.hit ? 35 : 14;
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(tx, 0, tx + t.w, 0);
      grad.addColorStop(0, '#221100'); grad.addColorStop(.5, '#ff7700'); grad.addColorStop(1, '#ffcc33');
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(tx, t.y + 14, t.w, t.h * .55, [0, t.w * .4, t.w * .4, 0]);
      else ctx.rect(tx, t.y + 14, t.w, t.h * .55);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#332200'; ctx.fillRect(tx - 6, GY - 14, t.w + 12, 14);
      if (t.hit) {
        ctx.font = '20px serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText('\uD83D\uDD25', tx + t.w * .8, t.y + t.h * .4);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = 'bold 14px monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('\u2192', tx + t.w / 2, t.y + t.h * .55);
      ctx.restore(); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    }
    else if (t.type === 'fly') {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ff88ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = t.hit ? 25 : 8;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#441166';
      ctx.fillRect(tx, t.y, t.w, t.h);
      ctx.fillStyle = t.hit ? '#ff88ff' : '#cc44ff';
      ctx.fillRect(tx + 2, t.y + 2, t.w - 4, 4);
      for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) {
        const bx = tx + b * (t.w / 3.5), by = t.y + Math.sin(frame * .12 + b * 1.4) * 7;
        ctx.fillStyle = `hsl(${270 + b * 18},100%,65%)`;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx, by, 7, 4, -.5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx + 10, by, 7, 4, .5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    else if (t.type === 'mud') {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#3a1500';
      ctx.fillRect(tx, GY - t.h, t.w, t.h);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#5a2a0a';
      for (let mx = 0; mx < t.w; mx += 12) ctx.fillRect(tx + mx + 2, GY - t.h + 3, 7, 6);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff5500'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('\u26A0 BOUE \u26A0', tx + 4, GY - t.h - 6);
    }
  }
}
function drawLauncher() {
  const lx = PIV_X - camX;
  const ly = GY;
  const pivX = lx, pivY = ly - PIV_Y_OFF;
  // Current arm angle
  let a = armAngle;
  const ex = pivX + Math.cos(a) * ARM_L;
  const ey = pivY + Math.sin(a) * ARM_L;
  ctx.save();
  // Socle arcade
  ctx.shadowColor = '#00f0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#000d1a';
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(lx - 42, ly - 78, 84, 78, 7);
  else ctx.rect(lx - 42, ly - 78, 84, 78);
  ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  // Ecran
  ctx.fillStyle = '#001830'; ctx.fillRect(lx - 28, ly - 72, 56, 32);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 6px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK FM', lx, ly - 59);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.font = '5px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('LAUNCHER', lx, ly - 50);
  // Boutons
  ['#ff4488', '#00ff88', '#ffe600'].forEach((col, i) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(lx - 16 + i * 16, ly - 22, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  });
  // Bras
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 10; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.shadowBlur = 25;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pivX, pivY); ctx.lineTo(ex, ey); ctx.stroke();
  // Contrepoids (bas du bras)
  const cwX = pivX - Math.cos(a) * (ARM_L * 0.4);
  const cwY = pivY - Math.sin(a) * (ARM_L * 0.4);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cwX, cwY, 18, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#aa0044';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cwX, cwY, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  
  // Coupelle
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey, 14, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  // Pivot
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pivX, pivY, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore(); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   DRAW CHARACTER — Formes variées par personnage
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawBottle(x, y, sz, rot, col, acc, shapeType) {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.rotate(rot);
  ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
  // Mapping shapeType → dessin
  const shapeMap = { round: 0, hex: 1, tri: 2, rect: 3, oval: 4 };
  const si = shapeMap[shapeType] !== undefined ? shapeMap[shapeType] : 0;
  if (si === 0) { // Round — forme goutte
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, sz * 0.3, sz * 0.7, sz * 0.9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, -sz * 0.55, sz * 0.3, sz * 0.45, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -sz * 0.9, sz * 0.22, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-sz * 0.2, -sz * 0.1, sz * 0.18, sz * 0.38, -0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  } else if (si === 1) { // Hex — hexagone
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const ang = (i / 6) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 6, r = sz * 0.82;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(ang) * r, Math.sin(ang) * r + sz * 0.22);
      else ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(ang) * r, Math.sin(ang) * r + sz * 0.22);
    }
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillRect(-sz * 0.22, -sz * 0.62, sz * 0.44, sz * 0.42);
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -sz * 0.88, sz * 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sz * 0.15, sz * 0.1, sz * 0.25, sz * 0.38, 0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  } else if (si === 2) { // Tri — chapiteau (tente de cirque, base large, sommet arrondi)
    // Corps principal : trapèze arrondi évoquant un chapiteau
    const topW  = sz * 0.32;  // largeur du sommet (étroit)
    const botW  = sz * 0.88;  // largeur de la base (large)
    const topY  = -sz * 0.92; // sommet
    const botY  =  sz * 0.85; // base
    const r     = sz * 0.14;  // rayon des coins
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath();
    // Bord gauche haut (coin arrondi)
    ctx.moveTo(-topW + r, topY);
    ctx.lineTo( topW - r, topY);
    // Arête droite montant vers le sommet (légèrement bombée)
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(topW, topY, topW + (botW - topW) * 0.5, topY + (botY - topY) * 0.5);
    ctx.lineTo(botW - r, botY);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(botW, botY, botW, botY - r);
    ctx.lineTo(botW, botY);
    ctx.lineTo(-botW, botY);
    ctx.lineTo(-botW + r, botY);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-botW, botY, -botW + (botW - topW) * 0.5, topY + (botY - topY) * 0.5);
    ctx.lineTo(-topW + r, topY);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-topW, topY, -topW + r, topY);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    // Bandes de chapiteau (rayures alternées)
    ctx.save();
    ctx.clip();
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    const stripeW = sz * 0.22;
    for (let s = -3; s <= 3; s += 2) {
      ctx.fillRect(s * stripeW - stripeW * 0.5, topY, stripeW, botY - topY);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // Étoile / pennon au sommet
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, topY - sz * 0.08, sz * 0.13, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = `bold ${Math.floor(sz * 0.18)}px sans-serif`; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('★', 0, topY - sz * 0.03);
    // Liseré bas
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.fillRect(-botW, botY - sz * 0.14, botW * 2, sz * 0.14);
    // Reflet
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-sz * 0.2, topY + (botY - topY) * 0.3, sz * 0.12, sz * 0.28, -0.3, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  } else if (si === 4) { // Oval → Bouteille arrondie (RETRO X)
    // Corps de bouteille : ventre large, col étroit, bouchon
    const bodyW  = sz * 0.68;
    const bodyH  = sz * 0.78;
    const neckW  = sz * 0.28;
    const neckH  = sz * 0.38;
    const capW   = sz * 0.34;
    const capH   = sz * 0.16;
    const bodyY  = sz * 0.22;   // centre du corps
    const neckY  = -sz * 0.52;  // bas du col
    const capY   = -sz * 0.90;  // haut (bouchon)
    // Corps principal (ellipse large)
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, bodyY, bodyW, bodyH, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    // Épaule arrondie (transition corps → col)
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, neckY + neckH * 0.5, neckW * 1.4, neckH * 0.9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    // Col
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, neckY - neckH * 0.1, neckW, neckH, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    // Bouchon (acc color)
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(-capW / 2, capY, capW, capH, capH * 0.5);
    else ctx.ellipse(0, capY + capH * 0.5, capW * 0.5, capH * 0.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
    // Étiquette sur le corps
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, bodyY, bodyW * 0.62, bodyH * 0.45, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    // Reflet vertical
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-bodyW * 0.3, bodyY - bodyH * 0.15, bodyW * 0.14, bodyH * 0.38, -0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  } else if (si === 3) { // Rect — rectangle arrondi
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-sz * 0.65, sz * 0.9); ctx.lineTo(sz * 0.65, sz * 0.9);
    ctx.lineTo(sz * 0.65, -sz * 0.2);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sz * 0.65, -sz * 1.05, 0, -sz * 1.05);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-sz * 0.65, -sz * 1.05, -sz * 0.65, -sz * 0.2);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -sz * 0.85, sz * 0.28, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
    ctx.fillRect(-sz * 0.65, sz * 0.1, sz * 1.3, sz * 0.22);
  } else { // fallback — round
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, sz * 0.3, sz * 0.7, sz * 0.9, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = acc;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -sz * 0.9, sz * 0.22, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
  }
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.restore();
}
function drawCharacter() {
  if (isAbsorbed) return;
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  const sz = c.size;
  const shape = c.shapeType || 'round';
  if (state === 'menu' || state === 'ready') {
    const tip = getArmTipPos(armAngle, false);
    drawBottle(tip.x, tip.y, sz, 0, c.col, c.acc, shape);
  } else if (state === 'swinging') {
    const tip = getArmTipPos(armAngle, false);
    drawBottle(tip.x, tip.y, sz, Math.sin(frame * .15) * .08, c.col, c.acc, shape);
  } else if (state === 'flying' || state === 'dead') {
    drawBottle(ch.x - camX, ch.y, sz, ch.rot, c.col, c.acc, shape);
  }
}
function drawParticles() {
  for (const p of particles) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = p.life;
    ctx.shadowColor = p.col; ctx.shadowBlur = 9;
    ctx.fillStyle = p.col;
    ctx.fillRect(p.x - camX - p.s / 2, p.y - p.s / 2, p.s, p.s);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MINIMAP (PICTURE IN PICTURE)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawMinimap() {
  if (state !== 'flying' && state !== 'dead') return;
  // Ne dessiner que si le joueur est hors-champ en hauteur
  if (ch.y > -100) return;
  const mmW = 240, mmH = 160;
  const mmX = 24, mmY = H - mmH - 24;
  ctx.save();
  // Fond et bordure
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,10,30,0.85)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#00f0ff';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(mmX, mmY, mmW, mmH, 8);
  else ctx.rect(mmX, mmY, mmW, mmH);
  ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  
  // Label
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff';
  ctx.font = '10px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('TACTICAL VIEW', mmX + 8, mmY + 16);
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(mmX, mmY, mmW, mmH, 8);
  else ctx.rect(mmX, mmY, mmW, mmH);
  ctx.clip();
  // On veut tout voir depuis le sol jusqu'à la hauteur du joueur
  // Le sol est à GY. Le joueur est à ch.y.
  const worldHeight = Math.max(800, GY - ch.y + 100);
  const scale = mmH / worldHeight;
  const worldWidth = mmW / scale;
  ctx.translate(mmX + mmW / 2, mmY + mmH - 10);
  ctx.scale(scale, scale);
  // Centrer sur le joueur en X, et caler le sol
  ctx.translate(-ch.x, -GY);
  // Sol
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1a0060';
  ctx.fillRect(ch.x - worldWidth/2, GY, worldWidth, 200);
  // Terrain
  for (const t of terrain) {
    if (t.x < ch.x - worldWidth/2 || t.x > ch.x + worldWidth/2) continue;
    if (t.type === 'glove') ctx.fillStyle = '#ee1111';
    else if (t.type === 'cannon') ctx.fillStyle = '#ff7700';
    else if (t.type === 'cloud') ctx.fillStyle = '#88ccff';
    else if (t.type === 'fly') ctx.fillStyle = '#cc44ff';
    else if (t.type === 'mud') ctx.fillStyle = '#5a2a0a';
    ctx.fillRect(t.x, t.y, t.w || 40, t.h || 20);
  }
  // Joueur
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  drawBottle(ch.x, ch.y, c.size * 2, ch.rot, c.col, c.acc, c.shapeType || 'round');
  
  ctx.restore();
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   ANGLE INDICATOR (replaces power gauge)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawAngleIndicator() {
  if (state !== 'swinging') return;
  const range = ARM_EQUI - ARM_REST; // de -95% à -50% = 45%
  const progress = (armAngle - ARM_REST) / range;
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress));
  // Optimal zone: 70%-95% (proche de la verticale)
  const good = pct >= .70 && pct <= .95;
  const ok = (pct >= .50 && pct < .70) || (pct > .95);
  const col = good ? '#00ff88' : ok ? '#ffe600' : '#ff4444';
  const gH = 200, gW = 20, gx = 32, gy = H / 2 - gH / 2;
  ctx.save();
  // Optimal zone bg
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,136,.10)';
  ctx.fillRect(gx, gy + gH * .30, gW, gH * .35);
  // BG
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0a0a0a'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#333'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.fillRect(gx, gy, gW, gH); ctx.strokeRect(gx, gy, gW, gH);
  // Fill
  const fh = gH * pct;
  const gr = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, gy + gH, 0, gy + gH - fh);
  gr.addColorStop(0, col); gr.addColorStop(1, col + '44');
  ctx.fillStyle = gr; ctx.fillRect(gx, gy + gH - fh, gW, fh);
  if (good) {
    ctx.shadowColor = '#00ff88'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,255,136,.25)'; ctx.fillRect(gx, gy + gH - fh, gW, fh);
  }
  ctx.restore();
  // Labels
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#888'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('TIMING', gx + gW / 2, gy - 10);
  ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  const label = good ? 'PARFAIT!' : ok ? 'BON' : (pct < .20 ? 'TROP TOT' : 'TROP TARD');
  ctx.fillText(label, gx + gW + 10, gy + gH - fh + 4);
  ctx.restore();
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   HUD
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawHUD() {
  const c = CHARS[selChar];
  ctx.save();

  // ── Bloc SCORE (haut-centre) ──
  const sW = 200, sH = 64, sX = W / 2 - sW / 2, sY = 10;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,15,0.7)';
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(sX, sY, sW, sH, 8); ctx.fill(); }
  else ctx.fillRect(sX, sY, sW, sH);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(sX, sY, sW, sH, 8); ctx.stroke(); }
  else ctx.strokeRect(sX, sY, sW, sH);
  ctx.shadowColor = '#00f0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 26px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText(score + ' m', W / 2, sY + 38);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,0.5)'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('DISTANCE', W / 2, sY + 16);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('RECORD: ' + best + ' m', W / 2, sY + 55);

  // ── Bloc PERSO (haut-gauche) ──
  const pX = 14, pY = 10, pW = 148, pH = 52;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,15,0.65)';
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(pX, pY, pW, pH, 8); ctx.fill(); }
  else ctx.fillRect(pX, pY, pW, pH);
  ctx.fillStyle = c.col; ctx.shadowColor = c.col; ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
  ctx.font = 'bold 11px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(c.name, pX + 10, pY + 22);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  if (maxAlt > 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#00ff88'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
    ctx.fillText('\u2191 ALT MAX: ' + maxAlt + 'm', pX + 10, pY + 40);
  }

  // ── Bloc PIQUÉ (bas-centre, bien séparé) ──
  if (state === 'flying') {
    const dvW = 230, dvH = 44, dvX = W / 2 - dvW / 2, dvY = H - dvH - 20;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,15,0.72)';
    if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(dvX, dvY, dvW, dvH, 8); ctx.fill(); }
    else ctx.fillRect(dvX, dvY, dvW, dvH);
    if (diveCooldownTimer > 0 && !canDive) {
      const pct = diveCooldownTimer / DIVE_COOLDOWN;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(dvX, dvY, dvW, dvH, 8); ctx.stroke(); }
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('PIQU\u00C9 \u2014 COOLDOWN', W / 2, dvY + 17);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#1a0010'; ctx.fillRect(dvX + 12, dvY + 24, dvW - 24, 8);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.fillRect(dvX + 12, dvY + 24, (dvW - 24) * (1 - pct), 8);
    } else if (canDive) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.8)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(dvX, dvY, dvW, dvH, 8); ctx.stroke(); }
      const pulse = 0.65 + Math.sin(frame * 0.1) * 0.35;
      ctx.globalAlpha = pulse;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
      ctx.font = 'bold 10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('[CLIC / ESPACE] \u2192 PIQU\u00C9 \u25BC', W / 2, dvY + 27);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    } else {
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#333'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(dvX, dvY, dvW, dvH, 8); ctx.stroke(); }
      ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('PIQU\u00C9 utilis\u00E9 \u2014 rebondis pour recharger', W / 2, dvY + 27);
    }
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MENU
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawMenu() {
  ctx.save();
  ctx.shadowColor = '#00f0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 28;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 24px Press Start 2P, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('JOYSTICK LAUNCH', W / 2, 68);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.font = '11px Press Start 2P, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('EASTER EGG #18', W / 2, 96);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#666'; ctx.font = '11px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('\u2190 \u2192 choisir  \u00B7  CLIC ou ESPACE pour lancer', W / 2, 122);
  /* ── Bouton plein écran ── */
  const fsX = W - 170, fsY = H - 100, fsW = 150, fsH = 34;
  ctx.save();
  const isFS = !!document.fullscreenElement;
  ctx.fillStyle = isFS ? 'rgba(0,255,136,0.12)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = isFS ? '#00ff88' : '#444'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(fsX, fsY, fsW, fsH, 6); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  else { ctx.fillRect(fsX, fsY, fsW, fsH); ctx.strokeRect(fsX, fsY, fsW, fsH); }
  ctx.fillStyle = isFS ? '#00ff88' : '#888';
  ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText(isFS ? '⛶ QUITTER PLEIN ÉCRAN' : '⛶ PLEIN ÉCRAN', fsX + fsW / 2, fsY + fsH / 2 + 3);
  ctx.restore();
  /* ── Archives button ── */
  const abX = W - 170, abY = H - 60, abW = 150, abH = 34;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = showArchivesPanel ? 'rgba(0,245,255,0.15)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = showArchivesPanel ? '#00f5ff' : '#444';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath();
  if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(abX, abY, abW, abH, 6);
  else ctx.rect(abX, abY, abW, abH);
  ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = showArchivesPanel ? '#00f5ff' : '#888';
  ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('\uD83D\uDCE6 ARCHIVES', abX + abW / 2, abY + abH / 2 + 3);
  ctx.restore();
  const cW = 128, cH = 148, gap = 12;
  const tot = CHARS.length * (cW + gap) - gap;
  const sx = W / 2 - tot / 2, cy = H / 2 - cH / 2 - 20;
  CHARS.forEach((c, i) => {
    const cx = sx + i * (cW + gap), sel = i === selChar, lk = c.locked;
    ctx.save();
    if (sel) { ctx.shadowColor = c.col; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; }
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? c.col + '1a' : '#0a0a0a';
    ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? c.col : '#252525'; ctx.lineWidth = sel ? 2 : 1;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(cx, cy, cW, cH, 9);
    else ctx.rect(cx, cy, cW, cH);
    ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
    if (lk) {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#333'; ctx.font = '28px serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('\uD83D\uDD12', cx + cW / 2, cy + 56);
    } else {
      drawBottle(cx + cW / 2, cy + 52, c.size * .9, 0, c.col, c.acc, c.shapeType || 'round');
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = lk ? '#3a3a3a' : (sel ? c.col : '#777');
    ctx.font = `${sel ? '8' : '7'}px Press Start 2P, monospace`; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    const nLines = c.name.split(' ');
    nLines.forEach((ln, li) => ctx.fillText(ln, cx + cW / 2, cy + 84 + li * 13));
    if (!lk) {
      ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.fillStyle = '#555';
      ctx.fillText('MASSE', cx + cW / 2 - 28, cy + 108);
      ctx.fillText('AERO', cx + cW / 2, cy + 108);
      ctx.fillText('REBOND', cx + cW / 2 + 28, cy + 108);
      statBar(cx + 8, cy + 112, (cW - 16) / 3 - 2, 5, c.mass / 1.8, '#ff4488');
      statBar(cx + 8 + (cW - 16) / 3 + 2, cy + 112, (cW - 16) / 3 - 2, 5, c.aero / 1.4, '#00f0ff');
      statBar(cx + 8 + (cW - 16) / 3 * 2 + 4, cy + 112, (cW - 16) / 3 - 2, 5, c.elas / .85, '#00ff88');
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#444'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText(c.desc, cx + cW / 2, cy + 120);
    }
  });
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  if (best > 0) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe600'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe600'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
    ctx.font = 'bold 13px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('\uD83C\uDFC6  RECORD : ' + best + ' m', W / 2, H / 2 + cH / 2 + 50);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}
function statBar(x, y, w, h, v, col) {
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1a1a'; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
  ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w * Math.min(v, 1), h);
}
function drawDead() {
  const fade = Math.min(1, (140 - deathTimer) / 35);
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = fade * .78;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#000'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.restore();
  if (fade < .45) return;
  const a = Math.min(1, (fade - .45) / .55);
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = a;
  ctx.shadowColor = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.font = 'bold 32px Press Start 2P, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('GAME OVER', W / 2, H / 2 - 60);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.shadowColor = '#00f0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 22;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 22px Press Start 2P, monospace';
  ctx.fillText(score + ' M', W / 2, H / 2);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  if (score > 0 && score >= best) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe600'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe600'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
    ctx.font = '11px Press Start 2P, monospace';
    ctx.fillText('\u2605  NOUVEAU RECORD  \u2605', W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '10px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('CLIC / ESPACE pour rejouer  \u00B7  \u2190 \u2192 pour changer de perso', W / 2, H / 2 + 92);
  ctx.restore(); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}
function drawInstructions() {
  if (state !== 'ready' && state !== 'menu') return;
  const lines = [
    '\u2460 CLIC ou ESPACE \u2192 libérer le contrepoids',
    '\u2461 CLIC ou ESPACE au bon moment \u2192 relacher le cube',
    '\u2462 CLIC ou ESPACE en vol \u2192 PIQUE (relance apres rebond)',
    '   Testez les Gants de Boxe pour un super rebond !',
  ];
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.55)';
  ctx.fillRect(W / 2 - 250, H - 120, 500, 100);
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,.3)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.strokeRect(W / 2 - 250, H - 120, 500, 100);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#888'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  lines.forEach((l, i) => ctx.fillText(l, W / 2, H - 100 + i * 16));
  ctx.restore(); ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   CANVAS LEADERBOARD — sous-catégories visibles + détails au clic
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function drawLeaderboard() {
  if (state !== 'menu' && state !== 'dead') return;
  const lbX = 16, lbY = 16, lbW = 260, lbH = 310;
  ctx.save();

  // ── Fond + bordure ──
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,8,18,0.92)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,0.4)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(lbX, lbY, lbW, lbH, 8); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  else { ctx.fillRect(lbX, lbY, lbW, lbH); ctx.strokeRect(lbX, lbY, lbW, lbH); }

  // ── En-tête ──
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('🏆 CLASSEMENT', lbX + lbW / 2, lbY + 17);

  // ── Onglets catégorie ──
  const cats = [
    { key: 'best',  label: '🏆 BEST',  col: '#ffe600' },
    { key: 'worst', label: '💀 WORST', col: '#ff4444' },
    { key: 'alt',   label: '↑ ALT',    col: '#00ff88' },
  ];
  const tabW = (lbW - 16) / 3;
  cats.forEach((c, i) => {
    const tx = lbX + 8 + i * tabW, ty = lbY + 24;
    const sel = LEADERBOARD._active === c.key;
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? c.col + '25' : '#111';
    ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? c.col : '#2a2a2a'; ctx.lineWidth = sel ? 1.5 : 1;
    if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(tx, ty, tabW - 2, 18, 4); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
    else { ctx.fillRect(tx, ty, tabW - 2, 18); ctx.strokeRect(tx, ty, tabW - 2, 18); }
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? c.col : '#555';
    ctx.font = (sel ? 'bold ' : '') + '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(c.label, tx + tabW / 2 - 1, ty + 12);
  });

  // ── Onglets période (DAILY / WEEKLY / MONTHLY) ──
  const periods    = ['Daily', 'Weekly', 'Monthly'];
  const pLabels    = ['DAILY', 'WEEKLY', 'MONTHLY'];
  const pTabW      = (lbW - 16) / 3;
  periods.forEach((p, i) => {
    const tx = lbX + 8 + i * pTabW, ty = lbY + 46;
    const sel = LEADERBOARD._period === p;
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? 'rgba(0,255,136,0.18)' : '#0a0a0a';
    ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? '#00ff88' : '#222'; ctx.lineWidth = sel ? 1.5 : 1;
    if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(tx, ty, pTabW - 2, 16, 4); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
    else { ctx.fillRect(tx, ty, pTabW - 2, 16); ctx.strokeRect(tx, ty, pTabW - 2, 16); }
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? '#00ff88' : '#555';
    ctx.font = (sel ? 'bold ' : '') + '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(pLabels[i], tx + pTabW / 2 - 1, ty + 11);
  });

  // ── Liste des scores ──
  const entries = LEADERBOARD.getAll(LEADERBOARD._active);
  const startY  = lbY + 70;
  const lineH   = 22;
  const maxVis  = Math.floor((lbH - 80) / lineH);
  const scroll  = LEADERBOARD._scroll[LEADERBOARD._active] || 0;
  const activeCol = LEADERBOARD._active === 'best' ? '#ffe600' : LEADERBOARD._active === 'alt' ? '#00ff88' : '#ff4444';

  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  if (entries.length === 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#444'; ctx.font = '9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('— Aucun score —', lbX + lbW / 2, startY + 36);
    ctx.fillText('Joue pour apparaître ici !', lbX + lbW / 2, startY + 52);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(maxVis, entries.length - scroll); i++) {
      const idx = i + scroll;
      if (idx >= entries.length) break;
      const e   = entries[idx];
      const ey  = startY + i * lineH;
      const isSelected = _lbSelectedEntry === e;

      // Fond de ligne (surbrillance si sélectionnée)
      if (isSelected) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,0.10)';
        ctx.fillRect(lbX + 2, ey - 1, lbW - 4, lineH - 2);
      } else if (i % 2 === 0) {
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.025)';
        ctx.fillRect(lbX + 2, ey - 1, lbW - 4, lineH - 2);
      }

      // Rang
      const medalCol = idx === 0 ? '#ffd700' : idx === 1 ? '#c0c0c0' : idx === 2 ? '#cd7f32' : '#555';
      ctx.fillStyle = medalCol; ctx.font = 'bold 8px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('#' + (idx + 1), lbX + 8, ey + 11);

      // Nom
      ctx.fillStyle = isSelected ? '#fff' : '#ccc'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
      ctx.fillText((e.name || '???').slice(0, 10), lbX + 32, ey + 11);

      // Personnage (petit)
      ctx.fillStyle = '#666'; ctx.font = '6px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
      ctx.fillText(e.char || '', lbX + 32, ey + 19);

      // Score
      ctx.fillStyle = activeCol; ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.font = 'bold 9px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
      ctx.fillText(e.score + 'm', lbX + lbW - 8, ey + 11);

      // Icône "cliquable"
      ctx.fillStyle = '#333'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'right';
      ctx.fillText('▸', lbX + lbW - 8, ey + 19);
    }
    if (entries.length > maxVis) {
      ctx.fillStyle = '#444'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('▼ ' + entries.length + ' scores (scroll)', lbX + lbW / 2, lbY + lbH - 5);
    }
  }

  // ── Panneau de détails si une entrée est sélectionnée ──
  if (_lbSelectedEntry) {
    _drawEntryDetail(_lbSelectedEntry, lbX + lbW + 8, lbY);
  }

  ctx.restore();
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}

// Panneau de détail d'une entrée (affiché à droite du leaderboard)
function _drawEntryDetail(e, px, py) {
  const dpW = 200, dpH = 180;
  // Si le panneau sort de l'écran à droite, le mettre à gauche
  const dpX = (px + dpW > W - 10) ? (16 + 260 - dpW) : px; // s'adapte
  const dpY = py;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,20,0.96)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(dpX, dpY, dpW, dpH, 8); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  else { ctx.fillRect(dpX, dpY, dpW, dpH); ctx.strokeRect(dpX, dpY, dpW, dpH); }

  // Bouton fermer en haut à droite
  const clX = dpX + dpW - 20, clY = dpY + 6, clS = 16;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.15)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(clX, clY, clS, clS, 4); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.font = 'bold 10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('✕', clX + clS / 2, clY + clS * 0.76);
  // Stocker la zone du bouton pour le hit-test (dans une variable globale)
  _detailCloseZone = { x: clX, y: clY, w: clS, h: clS };

  // Titre
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f0ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText(e.name || '???', dpX + 10, dpY + 18);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText(e.char || '', dpX + 10, dpY + 28);

  // Séparateur
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,240,255,0.2)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(dpX + 8, dpY + 34); ctx.lineTo(dpX + dpW - 8, dpY + 34); ctx.stroke();

  // Statistiques
  const stats = [
    { label: '📏 DISTANCE',  val: e.score + ' m',     col: '#00f0ff' },
    { label: '↑ ALTITUDE',   val: (e.alt || 0) + ' m', col: '#00ff88' },
    { label: '🎾 REBONDS',   val: e.bounces || 0,      col: '#ffe600' },
    { label: '💣 CANONS',    val: e.cannons || 0,      col: '#ff8800' },
  ];
  stats.forEach((s, i) => {
    const sy = dpY + 44 + i * 26;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#333'; ctx.fillRect(dpX + 8, sy, dpW - 16, 22);
    ctx.fillStyle = s.col + '22';
    ctx.fillRect(dpX + 8, sy, dpW - 16, 22);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#666'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(s.label, dpX + 14, sy + 13);
    ctx.fillStyle = s.col; ctx.font = 'bold 9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'right';
    ctx.fillText(s.val, dpX + dpW - 14, sy + 13);
  });

  // Date
  if (e.ts) {
    const d = new Date(e.ts);
    const dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR') + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    ctx.fillStyle = '#444'; ctx.font = '6px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(dateStr, dpX + dpW / 2, dpY + dpH - 8);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
let _detailCloseZone = null; // zone de clic du bouton ✕ du panneau de détails
function drawArchivesPanel() {
  const apW = 360, apH = 320;
  const apX = W / 2 - apW / 2, apY = H / 2 - apH / 2;
  ctx.save();
  // Fond
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,18,0.97)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#00f5ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(apX, apY, apW, apH, 10); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  else { ctx.fillRect(apX, apY, apW, apH); ctx.strokeRect(apX, apY, apW, apH); }

  // Titre
  ctx.fillStyle = '#00f5ff'; ctx.font = 'bold 11px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('📦 ARCHIVES MENSUELLES', apX + apW / 2, apY + 22);

  // Bouton ✕ fermer — haut à droite du panneau
  const clX = apX + apW - 30, clY = apY + 8, clW = 22, clH = 22;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.18)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,68,136,0.6)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  if (ctx.roundRect) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(clX, clY, clW, clH, 5); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke(); }
  else { ctx.fillRect(clX, clY, clW, clH); ctx.strokeRect(clX, clY, clW, clH); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ff4488'; ctx.shadowBlur = 6;
  ctx.font = 'bold 12px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
  ctx.fillText('✕', clX + clW / 2, clY + clH * 0.76);
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  // Stocker la zone pour le hit-test
  _archivesCloseZone = { x: clX, y: clY, w: clW, h: clH };

  // Séparateur
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,245,255,0.2)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(apX + 12, apY + 32); ctx.lineTo(apX + apW - 12, apY + 32); ctx.stroke();

  // Contenu
  const archives = LEADERBOARD._archives();
  const keys = Object.keys(archives).sort().reverse();
  if (keys.length === 0) {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '10px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('Aucune archive disponible', apX + apW / 2, apY + apH / 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#444'; ctx.font = '8px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
    ctx.fillText('Les archives se créent en fin de mois', apX + apW / 2, apY + apH / 2 + 18);
  } else {
    let cy = apY + 42;
    for (const k of keys) {
      if (cy > apY + apH - 28) break;
      const a = archives[k];
      // Titre mois
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe600'; ctx.font = 'bold 9px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('📅 ' + (a.month || k), apX + 12, cy);
      cy += 14;
      const bestTop = (a.best || []).slice(0, 3);
      if (bestTop.length > 0) {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#888'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace';
        ctx.fillText('BEST:', apX + 18, cy);
        bestTop.forEach((e, i) => {
          ctx.fillStyle = i === 0 ? '#ffd700' : i === 1 ? '#c0c0c0' : '#cd7f32';
          ctx.fillText(`${i + 1}. ${e.name}  ${e.score}m`, apX + 56, cy + i * 12);
        });
        cy += bestTop.length * 12 + 4;
      }
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#1a1a2e'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(apX + 10, cy); ctx.lineTo(apX + apW - 10, cy); ctx.stroke();
      cy += 10;
    }
  }
  // Légende bas
  ctx.fillStyle = '#555'; ctx.font = '7px Share Tech Mono, monospace'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('Cliquer ✕ ou ARCHIVES pour fermer', apX + apW / 2, apY + apH - 8);
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
}
let _archivesCloseZone = null; // zone de clic ✕ du panneau archives
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   MAIN LOOP
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function loop() {
  if (!gameRunning) return;
  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  frame++;
  // Trebuchet swing animation (Pendulum physics)
  if (state === 'swinging') {
    // Tension linéaire massive tirant vers ARM_EQUI pour un lancer surpuissant
    const torque = (ARM_EQUI - armAngle) * ARM_TENS;
    armSpeed += torque;
    armSpeed *= ARM_DAMP; // friction
    armAngle += armSpeed;
  }
  if (state === 'flying') {
    physics();
    tickP();
  }
  if (state === 'dead') {
    deathTimer--;
    tickP();
    if (deathTimer <= 0) {
      if (score > best) {
        best = score;
      }
      if (score > 10 || score < -10) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          const px = prompt(`\u2605 Score : ${score}m ! \u2605\nEntre ton pseudo pour le classement :`, "Joueur");
          if (px && px.trim().length > 0) {
            LEADERBOARD.add(px.trim(), score, CHARS[selChar].name, maxAlt, {
              bounces: bounceCount,
              cannons: cannonHitCount,
              gloves:  0,
              flies:   0,
            });
          }
        }, 10);
      }
    }
  }
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  drawBg();
  drawTrails();
  drawGround();
  drawTerrain();
  drawLauncher();
  drawCharacter();
  drawParticles();
  if (state === 'menu') {
    drawMenu();
    drawInstructions();
  } else {
    drawHUD();
    drawAngleIndicator();
    drawMinimap();
    if (state === 'ready') drawInstructions();
  }
  drawLeaderboard();
  if (showArchivesPanel && state === 'menu') drawArchivesPanel();
  if (state === 'dead') drawDead();
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   INPUT
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function onAction() {
  if (state === 'menu') {
    state = 'ready';
  } else if (state === 'ready') {
    state = 'swinging';
    armSpeed = 0;
  } else if (state === 'swinging') {
    doLaunch();
  } else if (state === 'flying') {
    // Piqué avec cooldown
    if (canDive && diveCooldownTimer <= 0) {
      isDiving = true;
      diveFrames = DIVE_DUR;
      canDive = false;
      diveCooldownTimer = DIVE_COOLDOWN;
      diveUsedCount++;
    }
  } else if (state === 'dead' && deathTimer <= 0) {
    reset();
  }
}
function onKeyDown(e) {
  if (!gameRunning) return;
  if (e.key === ' ' || e.code === 'Space') { e.preventDefault(); onAction(); }
  if (state === 'menu' || (state === 'dead' && deathTimer <= 0)) {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
      let n = (selChar - 1 + CHARS.length) % CHARS.length;
      while (CHARS[n].locked && n !== selChar) n = (n - 1 + CHARS.length) % CHARS.length;
      selChar = n;
      if (state === 'dead') reset();
    }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
      let n = (selChar + 1) % CHARS.length;
      while (CHARS[n].locked && n !== selChar) n = (n + 1) % CHARS.length;
      selChar = n;
      if (state === 'dead') reset();
    }
  }
}
let _keyDownRef, _mouseDownRef, _touchRef;

// Helper plein écran
function _toggleFullscreen() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('jfm-game-overlay');
  if (!overlay) return;
  if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
    const req = overlay.requestFullscreen || overlay.webkitRequestFullscreen || overlay.mozRequestFullScreen;
    if (req) req.call(overlay);
  } else {
    const exit = document.exitFullscreen || document.webkitExitFullscreen || document.mozCancelFullScreen;
    if (exit) exit.call(document);
  }
}

function attachInputs(c) {
  _keyDownRef = onKeyDown;
  _mouseDownRef = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const rect = c.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (c.width / rect.width);
    const my = (e.clientY - rect.top)  * (c.height / rect.height);

    // ── 1. Bouton ✕ du panneau de détails leaderboard ──
    if (_lbSelectedEntry && _detailCloseZone) {
      const z = _detailCloseZone;
      if (mx >= z.x && mx <= z.x + z.w && my >= z.y && my <= z.y + z.h) {
        _lbSelectedEntry = null;
        return;
      }
    }

    // ── 2. Bouton ✕ du panneau archives ──
    if (showArchivesPanel && _archivesCloseZone) {
      const z = _archivesCloseZone;
      if (mx >= z.x && mx <= z.x + z.w && my >= z.y && my <= z.y + z.h) {
        showArchivesPanel = false;
        return;
      }
    }

    // ── 3. Interactions leaderboard (onglets + clic score) — menu et dead ──
    if (state === 'menu' || state === 'dead') {
      const lbX = 16, lbY = 16, lbW = 260, lbH = 310;
      const tabW = (lbW - 16) / 3;

      // Onglets catégorie
      const catKeys = ['best', 'worst', 'alt'];
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const tx = lbX + 8 + i * tabW, ty = lbY + 24;
        if (mx >= tx && mx <= tx + tabW - 2 && my >= ty && my <= ty + 18) {
          LEADERBOARD._active = catKeys[i];
          _lbSelectedEntry = null;
          return;
        }
      }

      // Onglets période
      const periodKeys = ['Daily', 'Weekly', 'Monthly'];
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const tx = lbX + 8 + i * tabW, ty = lbY + 46;
        if (mx >= tx && mx <= tx + tabW - 2 && my >= ty && my <= ty + 16) {
          LEADERBOARD._period = periodKeys[i];
          _lbSelectedEntry = null;
          return;
        }
      }

      // Clic sur une ligne de score → sélection pour détails
      const entries = LEADERBOARD.getAll(LEADERBOARD._active);
      const startY  = lbY + 70;
      const lineH   = 22;
      const maxVis  = Math.floor((lbH - 80) / lineH);
      const scroll  = LEADERBOARD._scroll[LEADERBOARD._active] || 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(maxVis, entries.length - scroll); i++) {
        const idx = i + scroll;
        if (idx >= entries.length) break;
        const ey = startY + i * lineH;
        if (mx >= lbX + 2 && mx <= lbX + lbW - 2 && my >= ey - 1 && my <= ey + lineH - 2) {
          _lbSelectedEntry = (_lbSelectedEntry === entries[idx]) ? null : entries[idx];
          return;
        }
      }

      // Bouton ARCHIVES (menu uniquement)
      if (state === 'menu') {
        const abX = W - 170, abY = H - 60, abW = 150, abH = 34;
        if (mx >= abX && mx <= abX + abW && my >= abY && my <= abY + abH) {
          showArchivesPanel = !showArchivesPanel;
          return;
        }
        // Bouton plein écran
        const fsX = W - 170, fsY = H - 100, fsW = 150, fsH = 34;
        if (mx >= fsX && mx <= fsX + fsW && my >= fsY && my <= fsY + fsH) {
          _toggleFullscreen();
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    onAction();
  };
  _touchRef = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); onAction(); };
  document.addEventListener('keydown', _keyDownRef);
  c.addEventListener('mousedown', _mouseDownRef);
  c.addEventListener('touchstart', _touchRef, { passive: false });
}
function detachInputs(c) {
  document.removeEventListener('keydown', _keyDownRef);
  if (c) {
    c.removeEventListener('mousedown', _mouseDownRef);
    c.removeEventListener('touchstart', _touchRef);
  }
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   INIT / STOP
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
function initGame() {
  canvas = document.getElementById('jfm-game-canvas');
  ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  try { LEADERBOARD.checkAndArchive(); } catch(e) { console.warn('LB:', e); }
  resize();
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  gameRunning = true;
  reset();
  attachInputs(canvas);
  loop();
}
function stopGame() {
  gameRunning = false;
  if (rafId) { cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); rafId = null; }
  const c = document.getElementById('jfm-game-canvas');
  detachInputs(c);
  window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
}
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   EXPOSITION PUBLIQUE
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
window.JFM_GAME = { openGameOverlay, closeGameOverlay };
// Polyfill roundRect
if (typeof CanvasRenderingContext2D !== 'undefined' && !CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.roundRect) {
  CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.roundRect = function (x, y, w, h, r) {
    const rad = Array.isArray(r) ? r[0] : r;
    this.beginPath();
    this.moveTo(x + rad, y); this.lineTo(x + w - rad, y);
    this.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + rad);
    this.lineTo(x + w, y + h - rad);
    this.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - rad, y + h);
    this.lineTo(x + rad, y + h);
    this.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - rad);
    this.lineTo(x, y + rad);
    this.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + rad, y);
    this.closePath();
    return this;
  };
}
})();

# JS_FILES.md — JoyStick FM
> Dernière mise à jour : v8.7

Inventaire complet des fichiers JavaScript du projet.
Tous les scripts sont en mode `'use strict'` et chargés en **footer** (WordPress `wp_enqueue_script`).

---

## 📦 Ordre de chargement (footer.php / functions.php)

```
1. easter-eggs.js     (dépendance : aucune)
2. radio-player.js    (dépendance : easter-eggs.js)
3. main.js            (dépendance : radio-player.js)
4. dvd-bouncer.js     (dépendance : easter-eggs.js)
5. form-validation.js (chargé uniquement sur page-contact.php)
```

### `easter-eggs.js` — v8.7

Système complet de 18 easter eggs avec suivi de progression.

| Composant | Description |
|---|---|
| `EGG_TRACKER` | Module IIFE — suivi sessionStorage, mise à jour XP bar, pop-up finale 18/18 |
| `_playAudio()` | Lecture audio standard (desktop) |
| `_playAudioIOS()` | Lecture audio iOS via `fetch() + AudioContext.decodeAudioData()` (eggs 12 & 13) |
| `_patchIOSSlider()` | Patch touch events sur `<input type="range">` pour iOS |
| `_beep()` | Fallback synthétique Web Audio API si fichier MP3 manquant |
| `_bootAudioRegistry` | Gestion du son pendant l'écran boot (s'arrête à la fermeture) |
| `_onClick()` | Utilitaire multi-clic sur sélecteur CSS |
| `triggerKonamiEgg()` | Egg 1 — appelée depuis `main.js` |
| Eggs 2–18 | IIFEs autonomes avec listeners attachés au DOM |

**Easter Eggs implémentés :**

| # | ID | Déclencheur |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `konami` | `triggerKonamiEgg()` depuis main.js |
| 2 | `rickroll` | 5× clic `.logo` (hors `.logo-icon`) |
| 3 | `nyan` | 3× clic `.schedule-time` / `.schedule-hours` |
| 4 | `mario` | 5× clic `.schedule-show-title` / `.schedule-title` |

### `radio-player.js` — v6.5

Lecteur radio Icecast + mini-lecteurs podcasts + floating player persistant.

| Classe / Module | Description |
|---|---|
| `patchIOSSlider()` | Rend les sliders `<input type="range">` fonctionnels sur iOS |
| `formatTime()` | Formatage mm:ss |
| `PlayerRegistry` | Registre global — `stopAll()` garantit qu'un seul audio joue à la fois |
| `VolumeState` | Persistance du volume via `sessionStorage` |
| `SessionState` | Persistance de la piste podcast cross-page |
| `RadioState` | Persistance de l'état de la radio cross-page |
| `FloatingPlayer` | Barre audio fixe en bas de page — seek avec drag, gestion volume iOS |
| `RadioPlayer` | Lecteur Icecast — Web Audio API, Media Session API, Reverse Proxy |
| `MiniPlayer` | Mini-lecteurs podcast — fallback simulation si audio indisponible |
| `preloadDurations()` | Précharge les durées audio via `<audio preload="metadata">` |
| `PROGRAMME` | Tableau des émissions — mise en évidence automatique selon l'heure |

### `dvd-bouncer.js` — v1.0

Fonctionnalité décorative — logo DVD rebondissant en arrière-plan.

| Composant | Description |
|---|---|
| Canvas `#dvd-bouncer-canvas` | Canvas fixe `z-index:0`, `opacity:0.18`, derrière tout le contenu |
| Boucle `drawLogo()` | `requestAnimationFrame` — 1,4 px/frame, rebonds 4 bords, changement de couleur néon |
| `isInCorner()` | Détection coin (2 bords simultanés, tolérance 4px) |
| `triggerCornerEffect()` | Effet "Retour vers le Futur" : flash, lignes de vitesse, "88 MPH !", image + son |
| Overlay `#dvd-corner-overlay` | `z-index:99996` — flash, image `tk-dvd.jpeg`, texte "88 MPH !" |
| Animations CSS injectées | `dvdFlash`, `dvdLightning`, `dvdImgIn`, `dvdImgOut`, `dvdGlow`, `dvdSpeedLine`, `dvdMph` |
| `visibilitychange` | Pause automatique quand l'onglet est en arrière-plan (économie CPU) |
| `MutationObserver` | Attend la fin du boot screen avant de démarrer (page accueil) |
| Cooldown | 90 frames (~1,5s) empêchent le re-déclenchement immédiat |

**Configuration :**
```javascript
const SPEED_BASE  = 1.4;   // px/frame
const LOGO_SIZE   = 80;    // px
const CORNER_ZONE = 4;     // px de tolérance coin
const COLORS = ['#00f5ff', '#b44fff', '#ff2d78', '#39ff14', '#ffa500', '#ffff00', '#ff6b35', '#00bfff'];
```

### `form-validation.js`

Validation du formulaire de contact (chargé uniquement sur `page-contact.php`).

| Composant | Description |
|---|---|
| `RULES` | Règles de validation par champ (required, minLength, maxLength, pattern) |
| Validation temps réel | `blur` → validation, `input` → nettoyage erreur + compteur caractères |
| Soumission | Validation complète → `fetch()` vers Formspree → affichage succès |
| Honeypot | Champ `#website` caché — si rempli = bot détecté |
| Sons feedback | `_playErrorSound()` (sawtooth) + `_playSuccessSound()` (notes do-mi-sol-do) |
